Posts for 2014-09

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sun Aug 31 2014 19:22:50 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

'करनी न पूछो योगी की'

ऐसे बेरिस्टर के लिए आप क्या कहेंगे, जो सड़को पर क़ानून सम्मत बहस करता हो, लेकिन कचहरी में खामोशी की चादर तान लेता हो ?
इस विरोधाभास की तुलना ऐसे ऊँचे कद के नेताओं से की जा सकती है जो अपने साक्षात्कार और रलियों में किसी मुद्दे पर कर्रे वाले ऐलान देते हो, किन्तु संसद में सम्बंधित विषय का प्राइवेट मेंबर बिल पेश करने से कतराते हो ।
...

UP विधानसभा चुनावों के मद्देनज़र 'योगी' आदित्यनाथ भी वही कर रहे है ।
योगी महाराज का कहना है कि -
'पाकिस्तान में रहने वाले हिन्दूओ को भारत की नागरिकता दी जानी चाहिए'
अच्छी बात है !
लेकिन क्या उन्हें ये नागरिकता पेड मिडिया के माइक पर बोलने से मिल जायेगी ?
मुश्किल है !
.
प्रजा अधीन राजा समूह इस मांग का समर्थन करता है, लेकिन हमें लगता है कि ऐसा सिर्फ क़ानून पास करके ही किया जा सकता है , बयान बाजी से नही ।
समूह इस सम्बन्ध में कानून बनाने की मांग 2004 से ही कर रहा है, और हमने इस सम्बन्ध में लिखित कानूनी ड्राफ्ट दिया है । तथा इस विषय पर भी लिखित ड्राफ्ट दिया है कि पाकिस्तान से आने वाला नागरिक हिन्दू ही है कोई घुस्पेठिया नही, इसके निर्धारण के लिए आगंतुक का नार्को टेस्ट , ब्रेन मेपिंग तथा डीएनए टेस्ट हो, ताकि उसके हिन्दू शरणार्थी होने को सुनिश्चित किया जा सके ।
हमने प्रस्ताव किया है कि इन सभी मामलो की सुनवाई जूरी ट्रायल द्वारा हो ।
इन कानूनों का मुद्रित संस्करण 2012 से प्रकाशित हो रहा है ।
ड्राफ्ट देखने के लिए कृपया इस लिंक को देखे :
rahulmehta . com / 301 . h . htm
...

योगी महाराज चाहते है कि चुनावों के मद्देनज़र विवादित बयान देकर हिन्दू वोट खींचे जाए, और सरकार बनाई जाए ।
उनके कृत्यों से लगता है कि इस विषय में कोई क़ानून पास करने की उनकी मंशा नही है ।
यदि योगी आदित्यनाथ वाकई इस क़ानून को लाने के प्रति गम्भीर है , तो उनके पास इसका लिखित ड्राफ्ट होना चाहिए, और उन्हें इस ड्राफ्ट को कार्यकर्ताओं में वितरित करना चाहिए ।
.
क्या आप जानते है कि योगी महाराज ने पाकिस्तान के हिन्दू शरणार्थियों को भारतीय नागरिकता देने के निस्बत संसद में कितनी बार प्राइवेट मेंबर बिल पेश किया है ?
- शून्य, सिफ़र, जीरो, नन
.
चूंकि नमो और उनके डिप्टी शाह अब जिम्मेदार पदों की शोभा बढ़ा रहे है इसलिए वक़्त का तकाज़ा है कि अब वे दोनों विवादित बयानों के प्रसारण में सयंम बरते ।
नतीजतन योगी आदित्यनाथ आजकल फॉर्म में चल रहे है, और बयानों के उत्पात मचा रहे है ।
.
जहां मुस्लिम ज्यादा हो जाते है वहाँ पे दंगे ज्यादा होते है - योगी उवाच ।
लव जिहाद अन्तराष्ट्रीय साज़िश है - योगी उवाच

ठीक बात है । साज़िश है ।
परन्तु इस समस्या के समाधान के लिए योगी ने पिछले 100 दिन में या 20 वर्ष की सांसदी में कितने प्राइवेट मेंबर बिल पेश किये है ?
- अण्डा ।
...

असल में हमारे तमाम भारी भरकम नेता देश को संसद की जगह टीवी चेनलो के स्टूडियो से बयानों के माध्यम से ही चलाना चाहते है ।
- कारण ?
क्योंकि हमारे नेता जानते है कि नागरिक कानूनों में कम बयानों में ज्यादा, संसद की कार्यवाही में कम और पेड मिडिया के सर्कस में ज्यादा और समाधान में कम और समस्या में ज्यादा रुचि दिखाते है ।
...

समाधान : हमें नेताओं के बयान सुनने ही नही चाहिए, बल्कि यह देखना चाहिए कि अमुक समस्या पर नेताजी ने कितने क़ानून पास किये है अथवा समस्या के समाधान के लिए लिखित में कानूनी ड्राफ्ट दिए है या नही ।

प्रजा अधीन राजा @ pk

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Sep 01 2014 04:20:13 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

The format of "convince 42 crore voters to order their MPs via SMS" campaign to improve education or improve defence or "whatever".
.
1. The first step in "convince 42 crore voters to order their MPs via SMS" is to prepare the law-draft that you think will reduce one specific problem. eg we want to improve education in India, and that IMO needs about 20 law-drafts. One of the most important law-draft is Right to Recall District Education law-draft. The draft is given at " tinyurl .com/RahulMehtaOrder10 OR tinyurl .com/RtrEduOff " . . (remove spaces)
.
2 The next step is to write the draft on FB as status. The status post IMO should have two parts. One for citizen --- what exact SMS he should send. And another should be for MP -- what draft he is being ordered by voter to print in the Gazette.
.
3. Get premalink to your status post by clicking the time below your status.
.
4. Please see tinyurl , com , Please post the premalink of your status. And please make a good tinyurl code for your status. eg tinyurl codes I have made are RahulMehtaOrder8 , RtrEduOff, RtrDistJudge etc etc.
.
5. The tinyurl code should in the status post you made !! How to get that without editing post is a mystry :) . Please think.
.
6. Now please convince 42 crore voters across India to send that tinyurl code as order to MP via SMS . For that, you have to distribute pamphlets, give newspaper advt etc etc
.
7. Please explain to activists why SoMoKe etc oppose "convince 42 crore voters to send orders to MP via SMS" campaign.
,

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Sep 01 2014 13:28:10 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Vote Jihad (*) of BJP and Yogi Adityanath

Arun Jetley, in year 2001 passed a law which said that "if a man below 18 years commits any number of any serious henious crimes, then also punishment will be below 3 years" !! And Yogi Adityanath was MP back then, and he had voted YES on that law. This law is the singlemost important reason why violence against women increased against Love Jihad trippled in past 13 years. And in past 13 years, Adityanath has been MP for several years. And in those yearsm he never presented any private member's bill to reduce juvenile age from 18 years to 16 years !!
.
So this what MPs like Arun Jetley and Adityanath are. They will promote laws which increase problems to 10 times, and then run to collect votes on those problems !!
.
Solution I propose is --- the activists should ask for law-drafts these MPs have proposed in Parliament and should COMPLETELY disregard the tall speeches such MPs make . That will expose the reality of these "hot speech for votes MPs".
.

(* - the term Vote Jihad comes from Pawan Kumar. My sincere thanks to Pawan for coing this term. All credit to him)

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Mon Sep 01 2014 18:10:40 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

'बीजेपी और योगी का वोट जिहाद'

सन 2001 में वाजपेयी सरकार के कार्यकाल में माननीय मंत्री महोदय अरुण जेटली ने संसद में एक क़ानून पास किया था जिसके अनुसार :
'यदि 18 वर्षो से कम आयु का कोई नवयुवक किसी भी श्रेणी के किसी भी अपराध को चाहे कितनी भी दफा करे, उसे 3 वर्ष से अधिक सजा नही दी जा सकती, ऐसे युवक अपराधी को, चाहे उसने बलात्कार अपहरण और नृशंस हत्या की हो, कारागृह में नहीं रखा जा सकेगा, पुलिस द्वारा रिमांड पर लेकर पूछताछ नही की जा सकेगी तथा उसकी पहचान को सार्वजनिक नही किया जा सकेगा'
.
ज्ञातव्य है कि इस क़ानून के प्रभाव में आने से स्त्री के खिलाफ हिंसक अपराधो में भारी वृद्धी हुयी तथा लव जिहाद को पिछले 13 वर्षो में बड़े पैमाने पर बढ़ावा मिला । सजा पाए जाने का न्यूनतम जोखिम होने से 15 से 18 वर्ष के नवयुवको का हौसला बढ़ा, और दुष्परिणाम किशोरियों को भुगतना पड़ा । उल्लेखनीय है कि निर्भया काण्ड के मुख्य आरोपी की आयु भी 18 वर्ष से कम ( 17 वर्ष कुछ माह ) थी ।
राहत इन्दोरी याद आते है -

नयी हवाओं की सोहबत बिगाड़ देती है,
कबूतरो को खुली छत बिगाड़ देती है,
जो जुर्म करते है इतने बुरे नही होते मगर,
सजाएं न देकर अदालतें बिगाड़ देती है ।
.
जब संसद में इस क़ानून को प्रस्तुत किया गया था तब भी अरुण जेटली सुप्रीम कोर्ट के ख्याति प्राप्त दिग्गज वकील थे, तथा भगवा वस्त्र धारी आदित्यनाथ, योगी कहाते थे ।
इन्ही जेटली साहब ने इस बिल को प्रस्तुत किया था और इन्ही योगीराज ने इस बिल को पास करने के लिए 'हां' के पक्ष में मतदान किया था ।
.
पिछले 13 वर्षो से इस क़ानून की आड़ में महिलाओं के खिलाफ तमाम तरह के अपराध होते रहे, परन्तु योगी आदित्यनाथ ने इस क़ानून को समाप्त करने के लिए संसद में कभी प्राइवेट मेंबर बिल पेश नही किया ।
इस प्रकार इस समस्या को बढ़ने दिया गया, ताकि वक़्त आने पर इस समस्या पर बयानबाजी करके वोट खींचे जा सके ।
.
इस बात को कोई बच्चा भी समझ सकता है कि इस समस्या का बीज न सिर्फइनके द्वारा बोया गया बल्कि इसे फलने फूलने दिया गया, ताकि वक़्त आने पर इस मुद्दे पर वोटो की फसल काटी जा सके । यह सभी पार्टियो की रणनीति होती है कि पहले समस्या बढाने के लिए क़ानून बनाओ, और फिर उस समस्या का हल्ला मचा कर वोट बटोरो । जितनी ज्यादा समस्याएं, उतने ज्यादा वोट । क्योंकि हम इस बात को मानने को राजी नही है कि, योगी राज और जेटली महोदय को अंदेशा नही था कि, इस क़ानून को पास करने से लव जिहाद और बलात्कार की घटनाओं में बेतहाशा वृद्धि होगी ।
...

तो अब इन मुद्दों को क्यों जोर शोर से उठाया जा रहा है ?
^ क्योंकि बीजेपी राम मंदिर, धारा 370, समान नागरिक संहिता आदि समस्याओं को पिछले 25 सालो से नारों के रूप में उठा उठा कर वोट खींच रही है , और इन मुद्दों में अब करंट दौडाने की शक्ति नही रही ।
^ क्योंकि ये मुद्दे केंद्र सरकार के अधीन आते है, और नमो को इन समस्याओं को निपटाने के लिए बहुमत मिल चुका है ।
^ क्योंकि यह खुला रहस्य है कि विकास का मुद्दा वोट के लिहाज़ से दो कौड़ी का है, और केवल विकास के नाम पर UP विधान सभा में बहुमत नही जुटाया जा सकता ।
^ क्योंकि UP में 80 में से 73 सीट मिलने का मुख्य कारण हिंदुत्व का अंडर करंट तथा साम्प्रदायिक ध्रुवीकरण था, न कि सबका साथ सबका विकास का चूपा ।
^ क्योकि नमो ( मै जन्म से हिन्दू हूँ, और राष्ट्रवादी हूँ, इसलिए हिन्दू राष्ट्रवादी हूँ ) तथा उनके डिप्टी शाह ( मेरी हार्दिक इच्छा है कि अयोध्या में भव्य राम मंदिर बनना चाहिए ) अपनी बयानों की पारी आम चुनावों में खेल चुके है, इसलिए अब भगवा प्रतिक बना कर योगी आदित्यनाथ को अखाड़े में उतारा गया है ।
===

समाधान : कार्यकर्ता ऐसे नेताओं को बिलकुल भी बढ़ावा नही देवें जो कि समस्या बढ़ाते है और फिर उन समस्याओं को उछाल कर वोट बटोरते है ।
लम्बे चौड़े वादे करने वाले और मंच, रलियों, इंटरव्यू आदि में दहाड़ने वाले नेताओं के बयानों को अनदेखा करे और उनसे समस्या के निवारण के लिए लिखित कानूनी ड्राफ्ट देने के लिए कहें ।

Hi Prashant Rana ,
.
I am creating this thread for your comments/Q etc.
.
Dear All,
.
this thread is for comments of Prashant only. Others' comments will be deleted.
.
Hi Prashant ,
.
1. Our comments may be hopefully useful for other volunteers. So pls never delete a comment , unless lines of that comment are moved into other comment.
.
2. Pls combine CONSECUTIVE (only consecutive comments as follows)
.
2a. Pls click on edit comment-1
2b. Pls copy the content of comment-2 into comment-1
2c. Then pls delete the lower comment-2
.
3. Pls do NOT combine non-consecutive comments. PLEASE DO NOT COMBINE NON-CONSECUTIVE COMMENTS
.
4. To clarify what is consecutive comment and what is non-consecutive comment, let me give example. say
.
comment-1 is by you
comment-2 is by me
comment-3 is by you
comment-4 is by you
.
Then comment-3 and comment-4 are CONSECUTIVE comments and pls combine them
.
comment-1 and comment-3 are non-consecutive comments and so pls DO NOT combine them
.
5. Pls see my notes section on FB, and cntrl-F on SMS. There are notes on "citizens should order MPs via SMS". They are central to my campaign. Pls also download rahulmehta dot com slash 301.htm
.
6. Pls note that unlike other leaders, we NEVER ask activists to collect donations, hold our pictures, do naarebaazi etc. We only request activists to spread information about law-drafts, and we answers ALL questions for free. So I request ALL to putl comments and questions in PROPER textbook like Hindi or English, and NOT use SMS english, SMS abbreviation, and also not use informal writing style, as that makes reading time consuming and painful. Pls also avoid ALL CAPS posts.
.
7. If you have Q, pls first do ask Q to other volunteers. If you dont get satisfactory reply , then you are welcome to post it here. But before that, pls do ask Q to other volunteers.
.
8. Pls remember this thread's link by cntrl-D . In case you forget the link to this thread, please see "about me" -> "Full Info" on my page. There, you will see link to "two persons conversation threads" . Please search for your name there .
.
Thanx

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Sep 02 2014 04:07:55 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

NaMo/AY/BJP's Vote Jihad
.
There was a fire. Person-A poured 10 liters of petrol and person-B poured 3 liters of petrol. And so supporters of person-B say "see , person-B is less bad, and so lets make him in-charge of fire brigade"!! Thats how BJP supporters sound wrt problem of violence , love jihad etc in UP.
.
Let me give some details. Yogi Adityanath has been MP for over 15 years during 1996-2014. He was also BJP MLA in UP before that. And NaMo wasnt MP earlier, but he had 10s of MPs in pockets since 2002. Now in year 2001, it was Arun Jetley and ABV who printed a law-draft called JJA which said "Oh young men below 18 years !! No matter how many crimes you will do, no matter how henious crimes you do , the punishment will be less than 3 years !!"
.
This law-draft was the singlemost reason why Love Jihad and violence against women trippled in India since 2002. And Yogi Adityanath was MP in 2002 and he had voted YES on that law . And NaMo between 2002-2014 had enough MPs in his pocket to cancel the law or at least put a private member's bill in Parliament to amend it to 16 years or less. IOW, AJ, NaMo, YA , BJP-MPs etc did everything they could do to worsen the problem of violence. And now they come as solutions. Its like setting fire and then coming as fire-brigade. And worse, the dont intend to extinguish fire, but only collect votes !!
.
Let me add more to DELIBERATE inactions of BJP-apex, NaMo and AY. In their several years of MPship or having many MPs under them, none ever presented "2 child law" as a Govt bill or as a private member's bill to control the demographic jihad !! None proposed UCC draft as Govt bill or private member's bill. IOW, if the demographic jihad is going on , then it is due to deliberate inactions of Congress-leaders as well as BJP-leaders.
.
Now BJP-supporters say "see congress-leaders have poured 10 liters on fire, while BJP-leaders have poured only 3 liters on fire, And AAP-leaders will pour 20 liter if they get a chance" !! All true. All is very much true. But their next statement is a tragic comedy " and so BJP-leaders should be made in-charge of fire brigade!!".
..
=====
.
NaMo/AY's war crires and their decision to keep Indians wepaonless
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
.
Further, NaMo/AY have always opposed weaponization of us commons. Now most BJP-supporters also oppose weaponization of us commons. So they can rejoice over actions of NaMo/AY in keeping us commons weaponless. But many BJP-supporters do believe in a weaponization of us commons. Here too, these BJP-supporters use same logic "see Sonia\Congress are more responsible for keeping us Indians weaponless, while NaMo/BJP are less responsible !!" Well, both tried their best to keep Indians uniformed about need of a weaponized citizenry and dangers of weaponless citizenry. So one added 10 units of information blackout, and another added 3 units of information blackout. And so second one is "solution" !!
..
All in all, NaMo/AY's war cries on "love jihad" is nothing but pure "vote jihad". NaMo/AY will NEVER print any law-drafts to reduce the mess. They didnt do so in past decades, and will never do so now. What may happen is the dream of MNC-owners Missionaries will come true. -- a civil war, where most Indians are weaponless, and so will face en-mass slaughter.
.
Let me give details on possibility of coming civil war may turn into. Afghanistan and Pakistan are no friends as such. And Iran and Pakistan are more enemies than friend. But a civil war in India may end up uniting Saudi Arabia, Pakistan, Afghanistan and Iran against India, along with Bangladesh !! Now if we commons of India are weaponized, then (Pakistan + Iran + Afghanistan + Arabia) are NO problem for India. If all citizens have guns, we can deal with them, and even Chinese. If each Indian is as armed as a Swiss, then even if whole world attacks on us, we can can beat them.
.
But
.
(a) if we Indians remain weaponless , thanks to leaders leaders like SoMoKe,
.
(b) and if civil war happens and Pakistan/Afghanistan/Iran get united against Indians,
.
(c) then Indians will get slaughered en-masse. Because Pakistan has 2 crore AK-47s, Afghanistan has over 50 lakh AK-47s , while India has only 25 lakh AK-47s !!
.
(d) and then we will have only one option left --- touch feet of closest MNC-owners and Missionaries, and ask them to provide help and weapons !!
.
And thats what the game plan of YA = Yogi Adityanath AFAIS (AFIAS = as far as I see) --- keep Indians weaponless, and drive Indians towards civil war, so that the Indians are forced to touch feet of Missionaries for help.

YA does keep weapons with him, and more weapons to him. I have no issue with YA keeping weapons with him. But YA refuses to print law-drafts that will enable ALL Indians to bear weapons !! This is stark contrast between YA and a true sadhu, such as a Sikh Guru. Sikh Gurus kept weapons, and insisted that EVERY common man should keep weapons !! In ancient days, our Rishies also kept weapons, and asked EVERY person to keep weapons. While YA keeps weapons with himself and YA efuses to print law-drafts to enable all Indians to bear weapons. IOW, YA's intentions are malicious.
.
My point is --- let vote jihad go on. CoBhAp will do what they are made for --- conduct vote jihad in name of freedom or in the name of love jihad or in the name corruption. But these CoBhAp are NOT solutions to any problem they talk about. They will do all jihad for votes and then dump the issues. We should focus on solutions and we should not get swayed by these vote jiahds. Solutions are law-drafts that can reduce the mess. I have given the law-drafts I have proposed in my book at rahulmehta . com/301.htm . The rest are in my notes. I would request you to publicize these law-drafts, and NOT get swayed by vote jihad of SoMoKe and YA under variois causes.
.
(Vote Jihad term was coined by RTR-activist Pawan Kumar . All credit to him. Please follow him. He translated many of my status posts in Hindi)

NaMo's FDI Jihad .
.
SoMoKe, SoMoKe-andhbhagats and CoBhApSwaRa-workers have done something which British could NOT do in 200 years --- keep Indians uninformed about repatriation crisis FDI can create, how repatriation crisis can force us to sell away everything, how foreign investors can kill real internal growth and how FDI helps Missionaries !!.
.
FDI always leads to short term prosperity. Thats why when MMS went on FDI spree in 1990s, the prosperity had increased. And since then FDI-led growth indeed increases prosperity. But FDI-led growth does NOT increase skills. eg we do NOT manufacture even Rs 1000 simple call only dumb phone, while China manufactures MI3,
.
The betterment which happened in these 100 days too is due to more FDI only Due to FDI, dollar prices fell, and so petrol prices fell. There is no long story here. And now Japan is promising FDI of some $35 billion.. Those who think that Japanese MNC-owners are less bad than US-MNC-owners are mistaken. Please read on Nanjing war.
.
Also, it is fallacy that "US MNC-owners will help Missionaries while Japanese MNC-owners will NOT help Missionaries.". The Missionaries have NOT penetrated Japanese society, but have made deep inroads in Japanese political and economic elite. eg please google on religious affiliations of previous Japanese PM and existing Japanese PM's wife. The Missionaries will manage to get deep inroads via Japanese elietemen who are influenced by Missionaries.
.
One interesting change in Indian Politics of past 5 years is BST-workers. BST-workers were all anti-FDI in 2009 when BST started. Now almost all BST-workers are busy supporting FDI in defence, FDI in railways and FDI in everything, and they insist on supressing information on repatriation crisis FDI can cause !!
.
Solution I propose is ---- we should reduce FDI by law-drafts such as WOIC, and by reducing internal inefficiencies using law-drafts such as DDMRCAM, JurySys, Wealth tax etc . Please my notes for these law-drafts. WOIC is explained in rahulmehta . com\301.htm , Please search on WOIC.
..

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Sep 02 2014 12:32:59 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Dear, All,
.
This status post is for those voters of India and activists of India, who want PM to print DDMRCAM = Direct Deposit of Mineral Royalties in Citizens Accounts and Military law-draft in Gazette. please send following order to your MP via SMS --- "Dear MP, I order you to print tinyurl .com/DDMRCAM draft in the Gazette" .
.
(note - Please see comments for Hindi translation, explanation etc. And please dont post comments here. Comments posted will be deleted. If you have questions etc, please see my index notes first, and then post questions in my wall, not here)
.
====
.
Dear MP,
.
f you have received the internet link to this status via SMS from your voter, then it is order to you, to order CM to print the following DRAFT in Gazette, The Hindi translation is given in the first and second comments.
.
== start of draft of the proposed DDMRCAM draft ===
.
Section-1 : Registering citizens Approvals for NLRO candidates
.
(1.1) (general instruction) The word citizen would mean a registered voterThis GN will become effective only after over 37 cr citizens have registered YES on it.
.
(1.2) (procedure for PM) PM would appoint an IAS officer as NLRO (National Land Rent Officer) .
.
(1.3) (procedure for CS = Cabinet Secretary) If any citizen wishes to be NLRO, he may appear in person or place affidavit before CS. The CS is hereby ordered to accept his candidacy for NLRO after taking fee same as deposit amount for MP election. CS will issue him a serial number.
.
(1.4) (procedure for CS) The CS may assign above task to any class-1 officer.
.
(1.5) (procedure for Talati) A citizen can come in person to Talati’s office, pay Rs 3 fee and approves at most five persons for NLRO position. The Talati will enter his approvals in the computer and issue him a receipt with his voter-id#, date/time and the persons he approved.
.
(1.4) (procedure for Talati) The Talati will put the preferences of the citizen on Govt website as decided by the CS or Collector with citizen’s voter-ID number and his preferences.
.
(1.5) (procedure for Talati) If a citizen comes to cancel his Approvals, the Talati will cancel one of more of his approvals without any fee.
.
(1.6) (procedure for CS) On every Monday, CS may publish approval counts for each candidate.

Section-2 : Replacement of NLRO
.
(2.1) (procedure for PM) The word citizen would mean a registered voter of India
.
(2.2) (procedure for PM) If a candidate gets approval of over 50% of ALL registered citizen-voters (ALL, not just those who have filed their approval) in a district, then PM may expel the existing NLRO and appoint the person with highest approval count as NLRO.
.
(2.3) (procedure for PM) If the person on the seat has come by approvals, and the person with highest approval must have 2% more approvals than existing one, then and then only the PM will appoint the person with highest approvals for that position.
.
(2.4) (procedure for PM) If the person’s approval is below 33%, then PM may or needed not replace him with his appointee. But as long as approval is above 33% , PM need not replace him with his appointee. Discretion of PM will be final.
.
Section-3 : Ownership of plots under GoI
.
(3.1)(procedure for Supreme Court judges , High Court judges, PM, all citizens) The Citizens of India hereby decide and declare the plot of IIMA, plots of all IIMs and the plot of JNU as the property jointly and equally owned by the Citizens of India. These plots are NOT property of the State or the State of India or the Union of India or any other private/GoI party, but these plots are property of the Citizens of India. Further, all the plots of all UGC funded universities and colleges not owned by private companies or trusts are declared as the property of the citizens of India. All the officers and judges of India, including the PM, all the High Court judges and all the Supreme Court judges, are hereby requested NOT to admit any plea that opposes this decision and verdict of the Citizens of India.
.
(3.2)(procedure for SCjs, HCjs, PM, All citizens) All plots under following Ministries/Dept will come under NLRO
.
Ministry of Tourism
.
Airports, all buildings owned by Air India and Indian Airlines
.
IIMs, all UGC funded colleges and universities except those teaching science and engineering
.
Ministry Consumer Affairs and Public Distribution
.
Ministry of Human Resource Development
.
Ministry of Information and Broadcasting
.
Ministry of Information Technology
.
Ministry of Rural Development
.
Ministry of Small Scale Industries & Agro and Rural Industries
.
Ministry of Social Justice and Empowerment
.
Ministry of Textiles
.
Ministry of Tourism and Culture.
.
Ministry of Urban Development and Poverty Alleviation
.
Ministry of Youth Affairs and Sports
.
National Human Rights Commission (NHRC)
.
Planning Commission
.
NLRO will have NO jurisdiction over land plots owned by private persons or companies or trusts or land plots owned by State Govt or Cities or Districts. He will have no jurisdiction on plots used Military , Courts, Prisons, Railways, Bus Stations, Govt Schools till class XII and tax collection offices
.
(3.3) (procedure for PM, All officers) All IITs, NITs and IISc shall be made part of DRDO, and the DRDO director shall be the Chief Officer of these colleges or shall appoint Deputy Chief Officers in these colleges to run the day today operations. The colleges teaching science and engineering will come under Ministry of Science and will not come under NLRO.
.
Section-4 : Collection of rents from GoI owned plots
.
(4.1) (procedure for NLRO) For the unused land, NLRO will divide the land in plots of appropriate sizes as he seems most profitable. NLRO will hold auction for each plot. The conditions for auction will beo The lease will be for 5, 10, 15 , 20 or 25 years as decided by NLRO. The lease cannot be more than 25 years.o The bidders will give bids for monthly rent and bidding period which can be less than maximum lease period. So bids will be in (monthly rent , months lease) format. One person can submit multiple bids. Minimum lease period will be 12 months. o The weight of the bid will Monthly_Rent / log(Lease_In_Months). i.e. more the rent, higher the weight and longer the lease, lesser the weight.o The bids will be openo The NLRO will give the plot as per weight of the bids.o NLRO will charge 6 months rent or collateral as deposit. o the tenant will be free to evacuate land any day and stop paying any rent
.
(4.2) (procedure for NLRO) During the lease time, NLRO will revise the rent every 3 years based on % change in the land prices in the 1sq km area around that plot and % change in prime lending interest rate from the day the plot was leased and the day when rent revision occurs.
.
(4.3) (procedure for NLRO) After the lease time is over, NLRO will hold a fresh auction, where in existing lease holder will get benefitso his weight will get multiplied by 1.1 to 1.5 depending on number of years he has paid rent.o he may increase his bid within 3 months after auction is over. o the existing lease holder will get 20% to 50% the 6 months’ advance rent new lease holder is paying depending on number of months he had held the land.
.
(4.4) (procedure for NLRO) But if existing lease holder loses the auction, then he can move or sell the fixtures on that land. But he will need to vacate that land.
.
(4.5) (procedure for NLRO) If the plot is held by an existing entity, the entity will get 25% plus (25% * lease in months /300), maximum of 50% , bonus in the bid i.e. its bid will be multiplied with 1.25 to 1.50 , but no more.
.
(4.6) (procedure for NLRO) If the plot is currently being used and occupied , NLRO will take the mean land price in past 3 years of sale in 1 km area round the plot and decide the price of plot and set (market_price * prime_interest_rate/3) as yearly rent for next 10 years. The rents will be revised every 3 years. After 10 years, rules stated from clause-1 onwards of this section will apply
.
(4.7) (procedure for NLRO) NLRO will give 34% of rent collected to Defense Minister for the purpose of strengthening Military and providing weapons and weapon-use education to all citizens.
.
(4.8) (procedure for NLRO) NLRO will dispatch 33% of the rent collected every month to the citizens residing in the State for past 10 years with limit of twice the amount received by citizens of India in last year. NLRO will dispatch rest of rent collected every month to the citizens of India.
.
(4.9) (procedure for NLRO) One year after this law is passed, the rent a person obtains
,
(4.9.a) will increase by 33% if he has no kids
.

(4.9.b) will decrease by 33% if he has more than (2 daughters, 1 son) or (1 daughter, 1 son) or 2 sons or 3 daughters and in which youngest kid is born 1 year after the law is passed
.
(4.9.c) will decrease by 66% if he has more than (3 daughters, 1 son) or (2 daughters, 2 sons) or (1 daughter, 2 sons) or 3 sons or 4 daughters and in which youngest kid is born 1 year after the law is passed
.
(4.7) (procedure for NLRO) The rent paid will be 33% higher for men above 60 and women above 55 ; and will be 66% higher for men above 75 and women above 70.
.
(4.8) (procedure for NLRO) No rent shall be paid to child below 7 years ; the rent to citizens between 7 to 14 will be 1/rd the normal and between 14 and 18 will be 2/3rd of the normal rent paid. .
.
Section-5 : Collection of Mineral Royalties

(5.1) (procedure for All Dept Secretaries) All the Department Secretaries who are in-charge of mines or crude oil wells or collecting royalties from mines or crude oil wells are ordered to send the royalties collected to NLRO

(5.2) (procedure for NLRO) The NLRO shall divide the royalties amongst Military, the citizens residing in the State and citizens of India in the same ratio as Land Rent described in the Ordinance dealing with distribution of Land Rent

Setion-6 : Citizens’ voice
.
(6.1)(procedure for District Collector) If any citizen wants a change in this law, he may submit an affidavit at DC’s office and DC or his clerk will post the affidavit on the website of Prime Minister for a fee of Rs 20/- per page.
.
(6.2) (procedure for Talati or Patwari) If any citizens want to register his opposition to this law or any section or wants to register YES-NO to any affidavit submitted in above clause, and he comes to Talati’s office with voter-ID and pays Rs 3 fee, Talati will enter YES/NO and give him a receipt. The YES-NO will be posted on the website of the Prime Minister.

Read full post →

Minimum lease period will be 12 months. o The weight of the bid will Monthly_Rent / log(Lease_In_Months). i.e. more the rent, higher the weight and longer the lease, lesser the weight.o The bids will be openo The NLRO will give the plot as per weight of the bids.o NLRO will charge 6 months rent or collateral as deposit. o the tenant will be free to evacuate land any day and stop paying any rent
.
(4.2) (procedure for NLRO) During the lease time, NLRO will revise the rent every 3 years based on % change in the land prices in the 1sq km area around that plot and % change in prime lending interest rate from the day the plot was leased and the day when rent revision occurs.
.
(4.3) (procedure for NLRO) After the lease time is over, NLRO will hold a fresh auction, where in existing lease holder will get benefitso his weight will get multiplied by 1.1 to 1.5 depending on number of years he has paid rent.o he may increase his bid within 3 months after auction is over. o the existing lease holder will get 20% to 50% the 6 months’ advance rent new lease holder is paying depending on number of months he had held the land.
.
(4.4) (procedure for NLRO) But if existing lease holder loses the auction, then he can move or sell the fixtures on that land. But he will need to vacate that land.
.
(4.5) (procedure for NLRO) If the plot is held by an existing entity, the entity will get 25% plus (25% * lease in months /300), maximum of 50% , bonus in the bid i.e. its bid will be multiplied with 1.25 to 1.50 , but no more.
.
(4.6) (procedure for NLRO) If the plot is currently being used and occupied , NLRO will take the mean land price in past 3 years of sale in 1 km area round the plot and decide the price of plot and set (market_price * prime_interest_rate/3) as yearly rent for next 10 years. The rents will be revised every 3 years. After 10 years, rules stated from clause-1 onwards of this section will apply
.
(4.7) (procedure for NLRO) NLRO will give 34% of rent collected to Defense Minister for the purpose of strengthening Military and providing weapons and weapon-use education to all citizens.
.
(4.8) (procedure for NLRO) NLRO will dispatch 33% of the rent collected every month to the citizens residing in the State for past 10 years with limit of twice the amount received by citizens of India in last year. NLRO will dispatch rest of rent collected every month to the citizens of India.
.
(4.9) (procedure for NLRO) One year after this law is passed, the rent a person obtains
,
(4.9.a) will increase by 33% if he has no kids
.

(4.9.b) will decrease by 33% if he has more than (2 daughters, 1 son) or (1 daughter, 1 son) or 2 sons or 3 daughters and in which youngest kid is born 1 year after the law is passed
.
(4.9.c) will decrease by 66% if he has more than (3 daughters, 1 son) or (2 daughters, 2 sons) or (1 daughter, 2 sons) or 3 sons or 4 daughters and in which youngest kid is born 1 year after the law is passed
.
(4.7) (procedure for NLRO) The rent paid will be 33% higher for men above 60 and women above 55 ; and will be 66% higher for men above 75 and women above 70.
.
(4.8) (procedure for NLRO) No rent shall be paid to child below 7 years ; the rent to citizens between 7 to 14 will be 1/rd the normal and between 14 and 18 will be 2/3rd of the normal rent paid. .
.
Section-5 : Collection of Mineral Royalties

(5.1) (procedure for All Dept Secretaries) All the Department Secretaries who are in-charge of mines or crude oil wells or collecting royalties from mines or crude oil wells are ordered to send the royalties collected to NLRO

(5.2) (procedure for NLRO) The NLRO shall divide the royalties amongst Military, the citizens residing in the State and citizens of India in the same ratio as Land Rent described in the Ordinance dealing with distribution of Land Rent

Setion-6 : Citizens’ voice
.
(6.1)(procedure for District Collector) If any citizen wants a change in this law, he may submit an affidavit at DC’s office and DC or his clerk will post the affidavit on the website of Prime Minister for a fee of Rs 20/- per page.
.
(6.2) (procedure for Talati or Patwari) If any citizens want to register his opposition to this law or any section or wants to register YES-NO to any affidavit submitted in above clause, and he comes to Talati’s office with voter-ID and pays Rs 3 fee, Talati will enter YES/NO and give him a receipt. The YES-NO will be posted on the website of the Prime Minister.

== start of draft of the proposed DDMRCAM draft ===
.
Section-1 : Registering citizens Approvals for NLRO candidates
.
(1.1) (general instruction) The word citizen would mean a registered voterThis GN will become effective only after over 37 cr citizens have registered YES on it.
.
(1.2) (procedure for PM) PM would appoint an IAS officer as NLRO (National Land Rent Officer) .
.
(1.3) (procedure for CS = Cabinet Secretary) If any citizen wishes to be NLRO, he may appear in person or place affidavit before CS. The CS is hereby ordered to accept his candidacy for NLRO after taking fee same as deposit amount for MP election. CS will issue him a serial number.
.
(1.4) (procedure for CS) The CS may assign above task to any class-1 officer.
.
(1.5) (procedure for Talati) A citizen can come in person to Talati’s office, pay Rs 3 fee and approves at most five persons for NLRO position. The Talati will enter his approvals in the computer and issue him a receipt with his voter-id#, date/time and the persons he approved.
.
(1.4) (procedure for Talati) The Talati will put the preferences of the citizen on Govt website as decided by the CS or Collector with citizen’s voter-ID number and his preferences.
.
(1.5) (procedure for Talati) If a citizen comes to cancel his Approvals, the Talati will cancel one of more of his approvals without any fee.
.
(1.6) (procedure for CS) On every Monday, CS may publish approval counts for each candidate.

Section-2 : Replacement of NLRO
.
(2.1) (procedure for PM) The word citizen would mean a registered voter of India
.
(2.2) (procedure for PM) If a candidate gets approval of over 50% of ALL registered citizen-voters (ALL, not just those who have filed their approval) in a district, then PM may expel the existing NLRO and appoint the person with highest approval count as NLRO.
.
(2.3) (procedure for PM) If the person on the seat has come by approvals, and the person with highest approval must have 2% more approvals than existing one, then and then only the PM will appoint the person with highest approvals for that position.
.
(2.4) (procedure for PM) If the person’s approval is below 33%, then PM may or needed not replace him with his appointee. But as long as approval is above 33% , PM need not replace him with his appointee. Discretion of PM will be final.
.
Section-3 : Ownership of plots under GoI
.
(3.1)(procedure for Supreme Court judges , High Court judges, PM, all citizens) The Citizens of India hereby decide and declare the plot of IIMA, plots of all IIMs and the plot of JNU as the property jointly and equally owned by the Citizens of India. These plots are NOT property of the State or the State of India or the Union of India or any other private/GoI party, but these plots are property of the Citizens of India. Further, all the plots of all UGC funded universities and colleges not owned by private companies or trusts are declared as the property of the citizens of India. All the officers and judges of India, including the PM, all the High Court judges and all the Supreme Court judges, are hereby requested NOT to admit any plea that opposes this decision and verdict of the Citizens of India.
.
(3.2)(procedure for SCjs, HCjs, PM, All citizens) All plots under following Ministries/Dept will come under NLRO
.
Ministry of Tourism
.
Airports, all buildings owned by Air India and Indian Airlines
.
IIMs, all UGC funded colleges and universities except those teaching science and engineering
.
Ministry Consumer Affairs and Public Distribution
.
Ministry of Human Resource Development
.
Ministry of Information and Broadcasting
.
Ministry of Information Technology
.
Ministry of Rural Development
.
Ministry of Small Scale Industries & Agro and Rural Industries
.
Ministry of Social Justice and Empowerment
.
Ministry of Textiles
.
Ministry of Tourism and Culture.
.
Ministry of Urban Development and Poverty Alleviation
.
Ministry of Youth Affairs and Sports
.
National Human Rights Commission (NHRC)
.
Planning Commission
.
NLRO will have NO jurisdiction over land plots owned by private persons or companies or trusts or land plots owned by State Govt or Cities or Districts. He will have no jurisdiction on plots used Military , Courts, Prisons, Railways, Bus Stations, Govt Schools till class XII and tax collection offices
.
(3.3) (procedure for PM, All officers) All IITs, NITs and IISc shall be made part of DRDO, and the DRDO director shall be the Chief Officer of these colleges or shall appoint Deputy Chief Officers in these colleges to run the day today operations. The colleges teaching science and engineering will come under Ministry of Science and will not come under NLRO.
.
Section-4 : Collection of rents from GoI owned plots
.
(4.1) (procedure for NLRO) For the unused land, NLRO will divide the land in plots of appropriate sizes as he seems most profitable. NLRO will hold auction for each plot. The conditions for auction will beo The lease will be for 5, 10, 15 , 20 or 25 years as decided by NLRO. The lease cannot be more than 25 years.o The bidders will give bids for monthly rent and bidding period which can be less than maximum lease period. So bids will be in (monthly rent , months lease) format. One person can submit multiple bids. Minimum lease period will be 12 months. o The weight of the bid will Monthly_Rent / log(Lease_In_Months). i.e. more the rent, higher the weight and longer the lease, lesser the weight.o The bids will be openo The NLRO will give the plot as per weight of the bids.o NLRO will charge 6 months rent or collateral as deposit. o the tenant will be free to evacuate land any day and stop paying any rent
.
(4.2) (procedure for NLRO) During the lease time, NLRO will revise the rent every 3 years based on % change in the land prices in the 1sq km area around that plot and % change in prime lending interest rate from the day the plot was leased and the day when rent revision occurs.
.

.
(4.4) (procedure for NLRO) But if existing lease holder loses the auction, then he can move or sell the fixtures on that land. But he will need to vacate that land.
.
(4.5) (procedure for NLRO) If the plot is held by an existing entity, the entity will get 25% plus (25% * lease in months /300), maximum of 50% , bonus in the bid i.e. its bid will be multiplied with 1.25 to 1.50 , but no more.

Contents of all status --- only for academic reasons
.
Dear, All,
.
This status post is for those voters of India and activists of India, who want PM to print Jury System law-draft in Gazette of India.Please send following order to your MP, MLA via SMS --- "Dear MP\MLA , I order you to print the http://tinyurl.com/JurySys law-draft in the Gazette" .
.
(note - Please see comments for Hindi translation, explanation etc. And please dont post comments here. Comments posted will be deleted. If you have questions etc, please see my index notes first, and then post questions in my wall, not here. Also, this is draft for JurySys in lower courts. For drafts for JurySys in High\Supreme Courts , please see my notes on Jury System in Index)
.
====
.
Dear MP \ MLA ,
.
If you have received the internet link to this status via SMS from your voter, then it is order to you, to order PM\CM to print the following DRAFT in Gazette, The Hindi translation is given in the first and second comments.
.
= start of the Gazette draft bring Jury System in Lower Courts in India ==
.
Section-1 : Appointment and replacement of Jury Administrator
.
1. (procedure for CM) Within 2 days after passing this law, the CMs shall appoint one Registrar for entire State and one JA (Jury Administrator) per Districtt
.
2. (procedure Talati, Talati’s clerk) A citizen residing in a District can present his ID and specify the serial numbers of (at most 5) candidates he Approves for the position of Jury Administrator in his District. The clerk will enter the requests in the systems and give the receipt to the citizen. The citizen to change his choices any day. The clerk shall charge a fee of Rs 3/-
.
3. (procedure for CM) If any candidate is approved by highest number of citizen-voters and over 50% of ALL citizen-voters, the CM will appoint him as new JA for that District within 2 days. If any candidate is approved by over 25% of ALL citizen-voters and his approval count is 2% more than existing JA, the CM will appoint him as new JA within 2 days
.
4. (procedure for CM) With approval of over 51% of ALL citizen voters in that State, the CM can cancel clause-2 and clause-3 and appoint his own JA for 5 years.
.
5. (procedure for PM) With approval of over 51% of ALL citizen voters in India, the PM can cancel cluase-2, clause-3 and above clause-4 for entire state or some of the districts and appoint JA for 5 years.
.
Section-2 : Formation of Grand Jury
.
6. (procedure for JA) Using the voter list, the JA will, in a public meeting, randomly select 40 citizens from the voter-list of District, State or Nation as the Grand Jurors, from which he can exclude any 10 after interview so that finally there are 30 Grand Jurors. If the Jurors is appointed by CM or PM under clause-4 or clause-5 he may select up to 60 citizens and exclude 30.
.
7. (procedure for JA) In the first set of Grand Jurors, JA will retire the first 10 Grand Jurors every 10 days and select 10 more using random selection from voter list of District or State or Nation.
.
8. (procedure for JA) The JA cannot use any electronic device to select a number randomly. He will use the procedure detailed by CM. If CM has not specified the procedure, he will select as follow. Suppose JA has to choose a number between 1 and a four digit number - ABCD. Then JA will have 4 rounds of dice-throw for each digit. In a round if the digit he needs to select is between 0-5, then he will use only 1 dice and if the digit he needs to select is between 0-9, he will use 2 dices. The number selected will be 1 less than the number which comes in case of single-dice throw and 2 less in case of double-dice throw. If the throw of the dices exceeds the highest digit he needs, he will throw the dices again.. Example - Suppose JA needs to select a page in a book, which has 3693 pages. Then JA will execute 4 rounds. In the 1st round he will use 1 dice as he needs to select a number between 0-3. If the dice shows 5 or 6, he will throw the dice again. If the dice show 3, the number selected is 3-1=2, and JA will proceed to second round. In the second round, he needs to select a number between 0-6. So he will throw two dices. If the sum exceeds 8, he will throw the dices again. If the sum is suppose 6, the second digit selected is 6 - 2 = 4. Like that, suppose the dices in 4 rounds show 3, 5, 10 and 2. Then JA will select digits as (3-1), (5 -2), (10-2), (2-1) i.e. page number 2381. The JA should use different citizens to throw dices. Suppose the voter-list has B books, the largest book has P pages and all pages have N entries. Then using above method or method described by CM, JA will select 3 random numbers between 1-B, 1-P and 1-N. Now suppose selected book has less than that many pages or the selected page has fewer entries. Then he will again select a numbers between 1-B, 1-P and 1-N.
.
9. (procedure for JA) The Grand Jurors will meet on every Saturday and Sunday. They may meet on more days if over 15 Grand Jurors approve. The number must be "over 15", even when less than 30 Grand Jurors are present. The meetings, if happen, must start at 11am and last till at least 5pm. The Grand Juror will get Rs. 200 per day he attends. The maximum payment a Grand Juror can get for his 1 month term will be Rs 2000. The JA will issue the checks 2 months after a Grand Juror completes the term. If the Grand Juror is out of district, he shall get Rs 400 per day of stay and if he is out state, he shall get Rs 800 per day of stay. In addition, they will get Rs 5 per kilometer of the distance between their home and court. The CM , PM may change the compensation as per inflation. All rupee amounts written in this clause and this law use WPI given by RBI in Jan-2008 and JA can change the amounts every six months using latest WPI.
.
10. (procedure for JA) If a Grand Juror is absent on a meeting, he will not get Rs 100 for that day and may loose up to thrice his amount to be paid. The individuals who are Grand Jurors 30 days later will decide the fine.
.
11. (procedure for JA) JA will start the meting at 11am. The JA arrive in the room before 10.30am. If a Grand Juror fails to arrive before 10:30am, JA will not allow him to attend the meeting and mark him absent.
.
Section 3: Charging a citizen
.
13. (procedure for JA) If any person, be a private person or District Prosecutor, has complaint against any other person, he can write to all or some Grand Jurors. The complainer must specify the remedy he wishes. The remedy can be· obtaining possession of a property· obtaining monetary compensation from the accused· imprisoning the accused for certain number of years/months.
.
14. (procedure for JA) If over 15 Grand Jurors, in a meeting, issue an invitation, the citizen may appear. The Grand Jurors may or may not invite the accused and complainer.
.
15. (procedure for JA) If over 15 Grand Jurors declare that there is some merit in the complaint, the JA will call a Jury consisting of 12 citizens from the district to examine the complaint. The JA will select more than 12 citizens randomly, and send them summons to them, and of those who arrive, the JA will select 12 at random.
.
16. (procedure for JA) JA will ask the Chief District Judge to appoint one or more Judges to preside over the case. If the property in dispute is worth above Rs 25 lakhs or compensation claim is above Rs 100,000 and/or the maximum prison sentence is above 12 months, the JA will request Chief Judge to appoint 3 judges or else he will request Chief Judge to appoint 3 Judges for the case. The Chief Judge's decision on appointing number of Judges in the case will be final.
.
Section-4 : Conducting a trial
.
17. (procedure for Presiding Judge) The trial will go from 11am to 4pm. The trial will start only after all 12 Jurors and the complainer have arrived. If any party has not arrived, the parties who have arrived must wait till 4pm and then only they can go home.
.
18 . (procedure for Presiding Judge) The Judge will allow the complainer to speak for 1 hour, during which no can interrupt. Then Judge will allow the employee to speak for 1 hour during which no one can interrupt. Like this, the Judge will alternate case. The case will go on like this on every day.
.
19. (procedure for Presiding Judge ) The case will go for at least 2 days. On the 3rd or later, if over 7 Jurors declare that they have heard enough, the case will go on for 1 more day. If on the next day, over 7 out of 12 Jurors declare that they would like to hear more arguments, the case will go on till over 7 say that case should end.
.
20. (procedure for Presiding Judge) On the last day, after both parties have presented the case for 1 hour each, the Jurors will deliberate for at least 2 hours. If after 2 hours, over 7 Jurors say that they need no more deliberation, the Judge will ask each to declare his verdict.
.
21. (procedure for Grand Jurors) In case a Juror or a party does not show up or shows up late, the Grand Jurors after 3 months will decide the fine, which can be up to Rs 5000 or 5% of his wealth, whichever is higher.
.
22. (procedure for Presiding Judge) In case of fine, each Juror will state the fine he thinks is appropriate, and MUST be less than the legal limit. If it is higher than legal limit, the Judge shall take it as legal limit. The Judge will arrange the fine amounts stated in increasing order, and take the 3rd highest fine, i.e. fine that is approved by over 8 out of 12 Jurors, as the fine collectively imposed by the Jury.
.
22. (procedure for Presiding Judge) In case of prison sentence, the Judge will arrange the sentence lengths cited by Jurors which must be below the maximum sentence as stated in the Law accused is charged with breaking, in increasing order. And the Judge will take the 3rd highest sentence i.e. prison sentence approved by over 8 out 12 Jurors, as the prison sentence collectively decided by the Jury.
.
Section-5 : The judgment, execution and appeal
.
23. (procedure for District Police Chief) The District Police Chief or policemen designated by him will execute the fine and/or imprisonments as given by the Judge and approved by the Jurors.
.
24. (procedure for District Police Chief) If 4 or more Jurors do NOT ask for any confiscation or fine or prison sentence, the Judge will declare the accused as innocent and the District Police Chief will take no action against him.
.
25. (procedure for Accused, Complainer) Either party will have 30 days to appeal against the verdict in the State's High Court or the Supreme Court of India.
.
Section-6 : Protection of a Fundamental Rights of the Citizens .
.
26. (procedure for All Govt Employees) No Govt employee will impose any fine or prison sentence without consent of over 8 out of 12 Jurors of the Lower Courts, unless approved by the Jurors of High Courts or the Jurors or Supreme Court. No Govt employee will imprison any citizen for more than 24 hours without approval of over 15 out 30 District or State Grand Jurors.
.
27. (procedure for To everyone) The Jurors will decide the facts as well as intensions, and shall also interpret the laws as well the Constitution.
.
28. This GN will come into force only after over 51% of all citizens in India have registered YES and every SCj has approved this GN.
.
29. (procedure for DC) If a citizen wants to propose any change in this law, then the citizen can submit an affidavit demanding the change to District Collector or his clerk who will post it on the website of Prime Minister for a fee of Rs 20 per page.
.
30. (procedure for Talati aka Patwari) If a citizen wants to register his opposition to this law or any clause of this law or wants to register any support to affidavit filed in the above clause, then he may register his YES/No for a Rs 3 fee at Patwari’s office. The Patwari will note the citizen’s YES/NO and will also post the citizen’s YES/NO on PM’s website.
.
============= end of draft ====

If the Jurors is appointed by CM or PM under clause-4 or clause-5 he may select up to 60 citizens and exclude 30.
.
7. (procedure for JA) In the first set of Grand Jurors, JA will retire the first 10 Grand Jurors every 10 days and select 10 more using random selection from voter list of District or State or Nation.
.
8. (procedure for JA) The JA cannot use any electronic device to select a number randomly. He will use the procedure detailed by CM. If CM has not specified the procedure, he will select as follow. Suppose JA has to choose a number between 1 and a four digit number - ABCD. Then JA will have 4 rounds of dice-throw for each digit. In a round if the digit he needs to select is between 0-5, then he will use only 1 dice and if the digit he needs to select is between 0-9, he will use 2 dices. The number selected will be 1 less than the number which comes in case of single-dice throw and 2 less in case of double-dice throw. If the throw of the dices exceeds the highest digit he needs, he will throw the dices again.. Example - Suppose JA needs to select a page in a book, which has 3693 pages. Then JA will execute 4 rounds. In the 1st round he will use 1 dice as he needs to select a number between 0-3. If the dice shows 5 or 6, he will throw the dice again. If the dice show 3, the number selected is 3-1=2, and JA will proceed to second round. In the second round, he needs to select a number between 0-6. So he will throw two dices. If the sum exceeds 8, he will throw the dices again. If the sum is suppose 6, the second digit selected is 6 - 2 = 4. Like that, suppose the dices in 4 rounds show 3, 5, 10 and 2. Then JA will select digits as (3-1), (5 -2), (10-2), (2-1) i.e. page number 2381. The JA should use different citizens to throw dices. Suppose the voter-list has B books, the largest book has P pages and all pages have N entries. Then using above method or method described by CM, JA will select 3 random numbers between 1-B, 1-P and 1-N. Now suppose selected book has less than that many pages or the selected page has fewer entries. Then he will again select a numbers between 1-B, 1-P and 1-N.
.
9. (procedure for JA) The Grand Jurors will meet on every Saturday and Sunday. They may meet on more days if over 15 Grand Jurors approve. The number must be "over 15", even when less than 30 Grand Jurors are present. The meetings, if happen, must start at 11am and last till at least 5pm. The Grand Juror will get Rs. 200 per day he attends. The maximum payment a Grand Juror can get for his 1 month term will be Rs 2000. The JA will issue the checks 2 months after a Grand Juror completes the term. If the Grand Juror is out of district, he shall get Rs 400 per day of stay and if he is out state, he shall get Rs 800 per day of stay. In addition, they will get Rs 5 per kilometer of the distance between their home and court. The CM , PM may change the compensation as per inflation. All rupee amounts written in this clause and this law use WPI given by RBI in Jan-2008 and JA can change the amounts every six months using latest WPI.
.
10. (procedure for JA) If a Grand Juror is absent on a meeting, he will not get Rs 100 for that day and may loose up to thrice his amount to be paid. The individuals who are Grand Jurors 30 days later will decide the fine.
.
11. (procedure for JA) JA will start the meting at 11am. The JA arrive in the room before 10.30am. If a Grand Juror fails to arrive before 10:30am, JA will not allow him to attend the meeting and mark him absent.
.
Section 3: Charging a citizen
.
13. (procedure for JA) If any person, be a private person or District Prosecutor, has complaint against any other person, he can write to all or some Grand Jurors. The complainer must specify the remedy he wishes. The remedy can be· obtaining possession of a property· obtaining monetary compensation from the accused· imprisoning the accused for certain number of years/months.
.
14. (procedure for JA) If over 15 Grand Jurors, in a meeting, issue an invitation, the citizen may appear. The Grand Jurors may or may not invite the accused and complainer.
.
15. (procedure for JA) If over 15 Grand Jurors declare that there is some merit in the complaint, the JA will call a Jury consisting of 12 citizens from the district to examine the complaint. The JA will select more than 12 citizens randomly, and send them summons to them, and of those who arrive, the JA will select 12 at random.
.
16. (procedure for JA) JA will ask the Chief District Judge to appoint one or more Judges to preside over the case. If the property in dispute is worth above Rs 25 lakhs or compensation claim is above Rs 100,000 and/or the maximum prison sentence is above 12 months, the JA will request Chief Judge to appoint 3 judges or else he will request Chief Judge to appoint 3 Judges for the case. The Chief Judge's decision on appointing number of Judges in the case will be final.
.
Section-4 : Conducting a trial
.
17. (procedure for Presiding Judge) The trial will go from 11am to 4pm. The trial will start only after all 12 Jurors and the complainer have arrived. If any party has not arrived, the parties who have arrived must wait till 4pm and then only they can go home.
.
18 . (procedure for Presiding Judge) The Judge will allow the complainer to speak for 1 hour, during which no can interrupt. Then Judge will allow the employee to speak for 1 hour during which no one can interrupt. Like this, the Judge will alternate case. The case will go on like this on every day.
.
19. (procedure for Presiding Judge ) The case will go for at least 2 days. On the 3rd or later, if over 7 Jurors declare that they have heard enough, the case will go on for 1 more day. If on the next day, over 7 out of 12 Jurors declare that they would like to hear more arguments, the case will go on till over 7 say that case should end.
.
20. (procedure for Presiding Judge) On the last day, after both parties have presented the case for 1 hour each, the Jurors will deliberate for at least 2 hours. If after 2 hours, over 7 Jurors say that they need no more deliberation, the Judge will ask each to declare his verdict.
.
21. (procedure for Grand Jurors) In case a Juror or a party does not show up or shows up late, the Grand Jurors after 3 months will decide the fine, which can be up to Rs 5000 or 5% of his wealth, whichever is higher.
.
22. (procedure for Presiding Judge) In case of fine, each Juror will state the fine he thinks is appropriate, and MUST be less than the legal limit. If it is higher than legal limit, the Judge shall take it as legal limit. The Judge will arrange the fine amounts stated in increasing order, and take the 3rd highest fine, i.e. fine that is approved by over 8 out of 12 Jurors, as the fine collectively imposed by the Jury.
.
22. (procedure for Presiding Judge) In case of prison sentence, the Judge will arrange the sentence lengths cited by Jurors which must be below the maximum sentence as stated in the Law accused is charged with breaking, in increasing order. And the Judge will take the 3rd highest sentence i.e. prison sentence approved by over 8 out 12 Jurors, as the prison sentence collectively decided by the Jury.
.
Section-5 : The judgment, execution and appeal
.
23. (procedure for District Police Chief) The District Police Chief or policemen designated by him will execute the fine and/or imprisonments as given by the Judge and approved by the Jurors.
.
24. (procedure for District Police Chief) If 4 or more Jurors do NOT ask for any confiscation or fine or prison sentence, the Judge will declare the accused as innocent and the District Police Chief will take no action against him.
.
25. (procedure for Accused, Complainer) Either party will have 30 days to appeal against the verdict in the State's High Court or the Supreme Court of India.
.
Section-6 : Protection of a Fundamental Rights of the Citizens .
.
26. (procedure for All Govt Employees) No Govt employee will impose any fine or prison sentence without consent of over 8 out of 12 Jurors of the Lower Courts, unless approved by the Jurors of High Courts or the Jurors or Supreme Court. No Govt employee will imprison any citizen for more than 24 hours without approval of over 15 out 30 District or State Grand Jurors.
.
27. (procedure for To everyone) The Jurors will decide the facts as well as intensions, and shall also interpret the laws as well the Constitution.
.
28. This GN will come into force only after over 51% of all citizens in India have registered YES and every SCj has approved this GN.
.
29. (procedure for DC) If a citizen wants to propose any change in this law, then the citizen can submit an affidavit demanding the change to District Collector or his clerk who will post it on the website of Prime Minister for a fee of Rs 20 per page.
.
30. (procedure for Talati aka Patwari) If a citizen wants to register his opposition to this law or any clause of this law or wants to register any support to affidavit filed in the above clause, then he may register his YES/No for a Rs 3 fee at Patwari’s office. The Patwari will note the citizen’s YES/NO and will also post the citizen’s YES/NO on PM’s website.
.
============= end of draft ====

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Tue Sep 02 2014 17:51:05 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

'नमो का FDI जिहाद'

सोनिया, नमो, केजरीवाल तथा इनके अंधभक्त, वह कार्य कर रहे है, जिसे 200 वर्ष पहले ईस्ट इंडिया कंपनी करने में असफल रही थी । देश डॉलर के पुनर्भरण संकट में फंसता जा रहा है, जिसका निश्चित दुष्परिणाम यह होगा कि देश के बहुमूल्य खनिज़ संसाधन और राष्ट्रीय सम्पदाएँ नीलाम होगी, स्वदेशी उद्योग बंद हो जायेंगे और धर्मांतरण में तेजी आएगी ।
लेकिन कोई भी राजनेतिक पार्टी इस बारे में नागरिको को सूचित नही कर रही है ।
.
विदेशी डॉलर की आवक होने से डॉलर गिरा जिस से फौरी तौर पे पेट्रोल की कीमतों में कमी आयी, जो कि कुछ समय में फिर से पूर्व स्थिति में आ जायेगी । बस ये इतना ही है जिस से पेट्रोल सस्ता हुआ, और कुछ नही ।
अब जापान से 35 बिलियन डॉलर का निवेश लाने की बात की जा रही है । इस निवेश से लाक्षणिक तौर पे अस्थायी विकास दर बढ़ेगी और बाज़ार हिलोरे लेने लगेगा, लेकिन हमारे स्वदेशी उद्योगों को सरंक्षण देने वाले कानूनों के अभाव में निर्यात गिरेगा, और हमारी अर्थव्यवस्था फिर से औंधे मुंह गिरेगी ।
.
कुछ खुशफ़हम और अपूर्ण जानकारी धारको का मानना है कि, जापान की बहुराष्ट्रीय कम्पनियों की धर्मांतरण में कोई रुचि नही है तथा जापानियों द्वारा मिशनरीज़ को फंडिंग नही की जायेगी । जबकि सच्चाई यह है कि जापानी कम्पनियों के निवशको और विशिष्ट वर्गो में अमेरिकन MNCs की अंदरखाने अच्छी खासी घुसपेठ है, एवं इन्ही के द्वारा इन्हें निर्देशित किया जाता है । पुष्टि के लिए जापान के पूर्व पीएम तथा वर्तमान पीएम की पत्नी के धार्मिक एवं मिशनरीज़ से गठजोड़ की जानकारी के लिए गूगल करे । ( plz also google on nanjing war )
.
बजाय इसके कि हमारे देश में तकनीक पैदा करने के लिए जूरी ट्रायल और गणित विज्ञान में सात्य प्रणाली के कानूनों को गेजेट में छापा जाए, नमो धुंवाधार MNCs के लिए रेड कार्पेट बिछा रहे है। इस से हमारे बचे खुचे उद्योग भी नष्ट हो जायेंगे और हम हमेशा के लिए तकनीक के लिए अन्य देशो पर निर्भर रहेंगे । जूरी सिस्टम और सात्य प्रणाली के कानूनों के अभाव का ही ये नतीजा है कि आज हम 1000 रूपये मूल्य का साधारण मोबाइल फोन भी नही बना पा रहे है, जबकि चीन का MI3 भारत में आउट ऑफ़ स्टॉक चल रहा है ।
.
FDI को लेकर भारत स्वाभिमान ट्रस्ट का स्टेंड सत्ता परिवर्तन के साथ ही जिस तरीके से बदला है, वो शोध का विषय है । हालांकि स्वदेशी जागरण मंच और चूपावादी संघठन RSS ने भी सत्ता में आते ही FDI के प्रति जो सुर बदले है वो किसी जादू से कम नही है, फिर भी बाबा रामदेव के शार्प V टर्न ने इन U और M टर्न लेने में माहिर संघठनो को भी कायल कर दिया है ।
इन्ही बाबाजी ने मई 2014 से पहले FDI को जितना अकेले ही कोसा उतना तो RSS और जागरण मंच ने भी नही कोसा था ।अब ये सभी संघठन अपना स्वदेशी का नज़ला तेल, साबुन, सर्फ़, पेप्सी, कोक आदि पर उतार रहे है और रेल, रक्षा, बीमा आदि भारी भरकम क्षेत्रो में FDI पर चुप्पी साधे बैठे है ।
.
FDI के बारे में नमो द्वारा भ्रामक बयान दिए जा रहे है कि FDI के आने से तकनीक आएगी, रोज़गार के अवसर बढ़ेंगे और विकास होगा ।
अव्वल तो यह कि FDI से प्रतिस्पर्धा ही समाप्त हो जाती है, जब किसी क्षेत्र में स्वदेशी कम्पनियां बचेगी ही नही तो तकनीक लेगा कौन ? यह अनुभव सिद्ध है कि MNCs जहां भी जाती है एकाधिकार स्थापित कर लेती है ।
क्योंकि हम स्वदेशी इकाइयों को सरंक्षण देने के क़ानून पास नही कर रहें है इसलिए FDI से विकास की जगह विनाश होगा, MNCs द्वारा कमाए गए मुनाफे का भुगतान डॉलर में करने में असफल रहेंगे और हमारी राष्ट्रीय संपत्तियां नीलाम हो जायेगी ।

===

समाधान : जूरी ट्रायल और WOIC का ड्राफ्ट गेजेट में छापा जाए । इन कानूनों के ड्राफ्ट यहाँ से मुफ्त डाउनलोड करे -
rahulmehta . com / 301 . htm
सभी कार्यकर्ताओं से अपील है कि अपने क्षेत्र के सांसद को इन कानूनों के ड्राफ्ट गेजेट में छापने का ऑर्डर SMS द्वारा भेजे ।

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Wed Sep 03 2014 19:07:36 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

'बयानों के योगी, विवादों के नाथ'

2011 में हुए सेन्सस के भाषा और धर्म के आँकड़े सोनिया ने प्रकाशित नही किये । मई 2014 तक नमो और योगी आदित्यनाथ ने कभी भी इन आंकड़ो को प्रकाशित करने की मांग नही की, अब मोदी सरकार को 100 दिन पूरे हो चुके है, लेकिन नमो इन आंकड़ो को सार्वजनिक करने की जगह दबा रहे है ।
.
योगी आदित्यनाथ पिछले 15 वर्षो से सांसद है तथा इस से पहले विधायक रहे है , भगवा वस्त्र धारण करते है और इन दिनों भड़काऊ और विवादित बयान देकर कोहराम मचाये हुए है ।
यहाँ कुछ प्रासंगिक प्रश्न है, जिनके जवाब योगिनाथ से अपेक्षित है :

1. मिशनरीज़ द्वारा किये जा रहे धर्मांतरण को रोकने के लिए योगिनाथ ने क्या कानूनी ड्राफ्ट दिये है ?
2. भारत में MNCs के बढ़ते आधिपत्य को रोकने के लिए योगिनाथ ने क्या समाधान दिए है ?
3. उनके मुख्य विषय, मुस्लिम जनसँख्या को नियंत्रित करने के समाधान हेतु क्या उन्होंने कोई कानूनी ड्राफ्ट दिये है ?
4. लव जिहाद को रोकने के लिए उन्होंने क्या कानूनी ड्राफ्ट दिए है ?
5. क्या उन्होंने कभी सेन्सस 2011 के धर्म और भाषा के आंकड़े जारी करने की मांग संसद में की है ?
6. वो नागरिको को हथियार रखने की अनुमति का विरोध करते है या समर्थन ?
यदि समर्थन करते है तो, क्या उन्होंने इस विषय पर कोई कानूनी ड्राफ्ट दिया है ?
7. क्या उन्होंने इस सम्बन्ध में कोई अनुमान प्रस्तुत किया है, कि लव जिहाद से पीड़ित युवतियों की संख्या कितनी है ?
.
अजय सिंह उर्फ़ योगी आदित्य नाथ पढ़े लिखे व्यक्ति है, गणित में स्नातकोत्तर है । किन्तु लगता है कि पिछले कई वर्षो से उन्होंने लिखना छोड़ दिया है, क्योकि वो अपनी पूरी ऊर्जा बोलने में जाया कर रहे है ।
हो सकता है कि ये हमारा भ्रम हो, किन्तु उनकी वेबसाईट और सम्बंधित स्त्रोतों पर उक्त समस्याओं के समाधान के लिए कोई भी कानूनी ड्राफ्ट मौजूद नही है, उन समस्याओं का भी नही जिन्हें वे पूरे जोर शोर से उठा रहे है ।
जाहिर है वे सिर्फ बोलते है, जैसा कि नमो द्वारा उन्हें बोलने के लिए कहा जाता है , नतीजतन इन समस्याओं पर उनके इतने धुवांधार बयानों के बावजूद समस्याएं जस की तस है । योगिनाथ भड़काऊ और विवादित बयान देकर अपने भक्तो का अच्छा ख़ासा मनोरंजन करते है, और बदले में भक्त थोक के भाव में उन्हे वोट दान करते है ।
बहरहाल यदि उनके या नमो के किसी अंधभक्त के पास इन समस्याओं के समाधान के कानूनी ड्राफ्ट्स का लिंक हो, तो कृपया भेजने का कष्ट उठाये ।

प्रजा अधीन राजा @ pk

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Sep 04 2014 03:12:03 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Yogi Adityanath's vote jihad helps only Missionaries, and thats why paidmediamen are giving so much coverage to YA. Solution -- please focus on law-draft , and not on speeches.
.
I have been saying since 1990s that "never read statements of a leader ; only read the law-drafts he proposes and opposes" !! I request all to extend that on YA.
.
YA is hot on statement and zero when it comes to law-drafts, despite his being an MP since 1998 for past 15 years. IOW, YA only runs a vote jihad and nothing else. YA doesnt mean a word what he says. But this "jihad for votes and votes only" can do damage beyond elections..
.
This not new. All parties specialize in vote jihad. AK too only wanted votes in the name of anti-corruption, and never cared about decreasing corruption. Congress since 1885 ran freedom movement, while all along it only served British. BJP used Ram janam Bhoomi, population explosion, Bangladeshi issues etc to collect votes And BJP leaders didnt do anything on it. CoBhAp all beieve that "voters to hote hi he bewkoof banane ke liye"
i.e. "voters are meant to be made fool of". So cooking issues is nothing new for them.
.
But YA's statements and YA's opposing to weaponization of commons go beyond vote jihad.
.
YA is making statements which imply that "all muslims are criminals, all muslims are rioteers, all muslims are love jihadies, all muslims are .!@#$%^". In effect , YA is saying that "we all Hindus must treat each and every muslim as a criminals". YA doesnt say that "criminals should be punished". In fact YA has been MP for 15 years from 1998 to now, and he never proposed any law-draft as privet member's bill to improve law-order. Worse, Arun Jetley proposed Juvenile Justice Act in 2001, and YA had voted YES on it, and that law-draft increase crimes against women by three times, including LJ So reducing crimes was never agenda of YA. YA is throwing speeches which do not say "punish criminals" or "punish criminal muslims", but YA says along the lines of "punish all muslims". IOW, YA is giving calls for a civil war in India.
.
And how does that benefit Missionaries?
.
Well, please also look at other side of YA. YA has opposed weaponization of commons.[/b] So when a civil war he wants starts, the Bangladeshi, Pakistan, even Iran, Afghanistan, Saudi Arabia etc will send money. guns and volunteers to all parts of India from West Bengal to Gujarat and JK to TN. And China will sell all guns they need for Saudi money. Where will Hindus get guns from? Nowhere. SoMoKe, YA etc have opposed weaponization of commons since they were born. So most Indians have only nail cutters to fight with. So AY wants a civil war , with a pre-requisite condition, that Indians must have no weapons !! IOW, it would be a one sided slaughter and nothing else. This will force the losing side i.e. Hindus to touch the feet of MNC-owners and Missionaries, and ask for help. And the help with come with the book and the cross.
.
Summarizing, strategy of YA is ------- (i) keep majority weaponless (ii) drive them towards civil war by making statements that "all muslims are criminals" (iii) and thus make majority face a slaughter and accept the help from Missionaries.
.
This will help Missionaries [b]ONLY[/b]. And thats why paidmediamen are giving so much coverage to YA. What makes things worse is that NaMo is also following same line, by giving all help YA needs.
..
Solution I propose ---
.
(a) the activists should ask YA to give the law-drafts he has proposed in Parliament on issues like weaponization of commons, reducing LJ , reducing violence by Juveniles etc. The activists should also ask YA why YA supported Juvenile Justice Act from 2001-2014 which virtually gives license to criminals below 18 years to commit all rimes they want, And why is YA not demanding ORDINANCE to reduce the age to 16 years !! And why has YA never demanded law to reduce govt control on temples. That will show you all that YA is just hot air.and time waste.
.
(b) and activists should focus on FDI-less law-drafts that can reduce problems such as LJ, violence, riots and also prepare us for eventual attack by Pakistan etc IOW, activists should focus on massive weaponization of commons to deal with coming slaughters. Last thing activists should do is waste time in morning walks at shakha, slogan shouting, cap wearing, jhadu weilding, and vyakti puja

Thu Sep 04 2014 03:44:42 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

NaMo wasnt born yesterday. So to what extent is BJP etc "responsible" for mess we see today? About 25%. How? And whats the solution?
.
For all problems India is facing today, the NaMo-bhagats blame ONLY Congress and they show BJP\NaMo as blameless. Well, NaMo\BJP werent born yesterday. NaMo was born 60 years back and has been in politics for past 40 years, And BJP \ Jansangh has been in politics for past 65 years.
.
They had ample time to present private member's bills[ to fix India between 1951-now and 2002-now. And they had support of 10% to 22% voters, which is more than sufficient to start a mass movement to get those private member's bills passed even against the wish of ruling party. But the cold fact is that BJP MPs never proposed any private member's bills to fix things. And NaMo wasnt MP, has been having dozen MPs in his pocket since 2002. But his MPs too never presented any private member's bill to fix India.
.
And AAP MPs are wasting time too. None of the 4 MPs have moved any private member's bill to bring important changes such as weaponization of commons, reduce corruption in supreme judges, reduce corruption in high judges, increase number of courts etc etc.
.
All in all, the mess we see today is creation of BOTH, Congress leaders and BJP leaders. Congress had more MPs, and is surely more 5 times more responsible. But BJP leaders are also neck deep. .
.
Now many say "see A had poured 10 liters of petrol on fire, while B had poured only 2 liters of petrol on fire, and so fire brigade would be best run under B" !! Thats what BJP-sipporters. But moral of story is --- BJP-leaders have proved their lack of intentions by NOT proposing any private member's bill in past decades and decades, and not initiating any mass movement to get those bill passed. Head count in Parliament may increase strength, but cannot add intention. And that is visible in past 100 days. In past 100 days, all we have seen is FDI , FDI and more FDI. So problems that FDI and dollar flow can reduce for time being, like decrease in petrol price etc happened. But no only action we saw on legislative fronts was --- excuses for non-actions.
.
Solution I propose is that activists should stop SoMoKe-puja and YA-pooja. Instead, the activists should focus on law-drafts to weaponize all citizens of India so that we can deal with possibility of ISIS coming to India. And activists should on law-drafts to fix courts, such as bring Jury System , increase number of courts and Right to Recall judges. Waiting for tall leaders to act will be another waste of scarce time.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Sep 04 2014 04:07:00 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Law-draft for weaponization of commons.
.
Dear citizen of India,
.
fi you want weaponization of commons in India, then please send following order to MP via SMS "dear MP, I order you to print http://tinyurl.com/StrengthenIndia in Gazette".
.
Dear MP,
.
If you got the link to this post via SMS, then it is an order from citizen to you to print this text in Gazette of of India.
.
=== start of draft ====
.
1. The Govt of India will put the design of INSAS rifle, 303, 202, .22 revolver and all guns used by Indian police , whose level is "below INSAS" in public domain. (INSAS is Indian version of AK-47)
.
2. Anyone , without need of license, by just registration , can start a factory which makes a gun or its parts or its bullets , with the above design. Anyone may also start factory to make bullet proof jackets.
.
3. If any citizen or govt officer believe that the factory owner is violating any law, then the case will be decided not by the judge , but by the Jury
.
4. Any citizen, unless banned by a Jury, will be allowed to possess the guns mentioned in clause-1 and its bullets, and also bullet proof jackets.
.
====== end of draft ======
.
To citizens,
.
This law-draft will enable every to bear INSAS level guns. This will enable us to deal with coming crisis, be ISIS or whatever.
.
Please keep in mind, that Pakistan has over 2 crore AK-47 level guns, Afghanistan has 1 crore such guns, Iran has factories to make guns etc. And India has only 25 lakh AK-47. And China can make and ship crores of AK-47 guns to Pakistan, Bangladesh etc. So earlier we improve our LOCAL gun manufacturing, without FDI (please note, WITHOUT FDI) the better. So if you like above draft, please send "dear MP, I order you to print http://tinyurl.com/StrengthenIndia draft in Gazette". as order to your MP via SMS.

Read full post →

1. The Govt of India will put the design of INSAS rifle, 303, 202, .22 revolver and all guns used by Indian police , whose level is "below INSAS" in public domain. (INSAS is Indian version of AK-47)
.
2. Anyone , without need of license, by just registration , can start a factory which makes a gun or its parts or its bullets , with the above design. Anyone may also start factory to make bullet proof jackets.
.
3. If any citizen or govt officer believe that the factory owner is violating any law, then the case will be decided not by the judge , but by the Jury
.
4. Any citizen, unless banned by a Jury, will be allowed to possess the guns mentioned in clause-1 and its bullets, and also bullet proof jackets.
.
====== end of draft ======
.
To citizens,
.
This law-draft will enable every to bear INSAS level guns. This will enable us to deal with coming crisis, be ISIS or whatever.
.
Please keep in mind, that Pakistan has over 2 crore AK-47 level guns, Afghanistan has 1 crore such guns, Iran has factories to make guns etc. And India has only 25 lakh AK-47. And China can make and ship crores of AK-47 guns to Pakistan, Bangladesh etc. So earlier we improve our LOCAL gun manufacturing, without FDI (please note, WITHOUT FDI) the better. So if you like above draft, please send "dear MP, I order you to print http://tinyurl.com/StrengthenIndia draft in Gazette". as order to your MP via SMS.
