Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Sep 15 2014 02:31:46 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Paidmedia says ---- Chinese enter 500 meters inside border, set up tents, put "this is our land" flag and also stop water supply !! Much of the weakness of Indian Military and local weapon manufacturing is weak due to past regimes. But to a small extent, the present regime is also responsible ---- they since 1951 opposed law-drafts needed to improve courts, improve industry and thus improve Military. .... SOLUTION?
.
Please google "Chinese Ladakh" and "Chinese Ladakh 500 tent", "Chinese Ladhak water supply" etc etc Please see links in the first comment. The paidmedia is full of news that Chinese have intruded in Ladakh area.
.
Much of the blame goes to previous regimes. USA had made PVNR PM of India and MMS the FinMin of India on the conditions that they would ruin Indian Military , manufacturing and also agriculture. PVNR was shakuni, not a chanakya as paidmedia often says. Later, ABV between 1996-1998 tried to improve India, but after USA won Kargil war, and India\Pakistan both lost Kargil war, ABV gave up. And Sonia-MMS too implemented the US proposals to weaken Indian Military, weaken local weapon manufacturing etc etc. so that we become more and more dependent on foreign countries for defence.
.
But present BJP regime has 80-140 MPs, from 1989-1996, 2004-2014 when Congress was in power. And BJP's last incarnation Jansangh has been having MPs in Parliament since 1951.
.
All BJP MPs never proposed any private member's bill to improve courts, improve manufacturing etc, And BJP MPs had also supported Jawahar Ghazi's proposal to kill Jury System in India in 1956, which led to weakening of Indian industries, BJP MPs also never moved any private member's bills to abolish cumbersome taxes like excise, CST, sales tax, VAT, octroi etc. And even during their six year regime, BJP MPs refuse dto improve court, print law-drafts to reduce land prices such as wealth tax, inheritance tax, abolish complex taxes like VAT, CST, excise etc. And since 1991 BJP MPs have been supporting laws like "Mauritius companies will pay no taxes and so forth".
.
Even now, BJP MPs including NaMo refused to print any law-draft to improve courts, abolish excise, abolish CST, abolish VAT etc and instead move to regime based on wealth tax, inheritance tax, income tax with no exemptions like SEZ etc. BJP MPs and NaMo also support laws such "Mauritius companies will pay no taxes", which further weaken Indian manufacturers.
.
NaMo was never an MP. But he has been having over a dozen MPs under him since 2002. And NaMo refused to ask MPs to submit necessary private member's bills to fix the courts, tax system, reduce land prices, shift clerical burden from factory owners to Govt and so forth. And the trend goes on. Past 100 days show major changes such as 100% FDI in defence, 100% FDI in railways, inviting Japanese to make bullet train and so forth, but saw no law-drafts to fix rampant nepotism in supreme\high\lower courts, now law-drafts to reduce tax load on factory owners and shift them to land hoarders, reduce clerical workload on factory owners to Govt and so forth.
.
And as of now, AAP MPs and AK are also opposing the proposals to submit necessary private members bill to improve courts, reduce tax loads on factory owners and shift them on to land hoarders, move clerical burden from factory owners to Govt etc etc. They insist that voters should give them 273 seats in Lokasabha before they will even show the draft of the bills !! And then next excuse will be "see , we dont have majority in Rajyasabha".
.
So while Congress regimes of are most responsible, the existing BJP MPs including NaMo are to a lesser extent responsible for present state of affairs. Their refusal to move necessary private member's bills to fix the system is the proof.
.
Solution?
.
Solution I propose to activists is that they should come out of SoMoKe-andbhakati and focus on law-drafts needed to improve courts, reduce land prices, reduce clerical load on factory owners and reduce arbitrariness of pollution control officers and so forth. More the activists waste time in SoMoKe-andhbhakti, slogan shouting, rallying etc, more will the increase in the gap between China and India's strength. Finally, only solution left to us to deal with Chinese threat will be --- accept all conditions of USA !! Which will lead to bigger set of weaknesses.
.
I at Right to recall Group has suggested law-drafts that can reduce weaknesses in rahulmehta . com/301.htm . As far as I know, CoBhApSwaRa-workers have opposed all law-drafts and insisted on neta pujan, andhbhakti, slogan shouting etc only. You decide which group you want to follow.
.

Finally, only solution left to us to deal with Chinese threat will be --- accept all conditions of USA !! Which will lead to bigger set of weaknesses.

And then next excuse will be "see , we dont have majority in Rajyasabha".

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Sep 15 2014 04:06:56 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

We at Right to Recall Group were the first and only ones to oppose "dont fail students till 8th class" policy and we proposed the solution. As our proposals became more and more visible, BJP leader Gulabchand Kataria moves forward to hijack , and DESTROY the issue.
.
The policy of "dont fail students till 8th class" started in around year 2005 gradually. We at Right to Recall Group have been opposing it since then. No Congress, no BJP and no AAP MP ever opposed it, They have always supported this policy till date. And we were the first one to say that this policy is coming because foreigners are bribing Ministers to bring this policy.
.
Now more and more people started knowing about this "dont fail till 8th" policy. And more and more people saw that this policy was wrong, and then more and more people also saw that we are the ONLY people opposing it. So our visibility started growing. Just as our visibility had started growing when we demanded that juvenile age should be decreased from 18 years to 14 years for crimes against women.
.
So to hijack, and destroy, the issue, BJP leaders have finally started speaking against this "no fail till 8th policy" !! Just as BJP MPs also started talking about reducing juvenile age from 18 years to 16 years. We in jan-2013 started asking voters to send orders to MPs via SMS to change juvenile age. Some 10000 voters across India had sent SMS to their MPs. BJP saw that as this movement grows, we will get more and more visibility. And so BJP-MPs hijacked this issue, but destroyed it !!
.
Now is this an attempt to "hijack and destroy" or "hijack and extend"? Let me explain.
.
Hijacking issue is not new. Apple hijacked Xerox's GUI concept, Later, Microsoft hijacked it from Apple. Chinese keep hijacking = copying all technology from world. But all these are cases of hijack and extend, i.e. hijacker improved the technology or made it cheaper or made it more available.
.
In politics, hijack and destroy is more common. eg BJP hijacked and destroyed the Swadeshi movement that was started by Rajiv Dixitjee etc in 1990s. BJP hijacked and destroyed the movement we had started to decrease juvenile age. How? NaMo made a proposal in cabinet and paidmedia wrote as it NaMo has already changed the law !! And so activists got misled by paidmedia and stopped asking voters to send orders to MPs via SMS to reduce juvenile age. They thought that law has changed and so they stopped asking voters to send SMS. IOW, there is no change in the law yet. If BJP MPs were sincere about changing the law, then they would have brought ordinance. Instead, they decided timepass way to send the bill to committees !!
.
Same way, BJP leaders are now opposing "no fail till 8th class" law. But but what law-drafts did they give to fix education ? NONE. BJP, like AAP and Congress, had been opposing law-drafts such as Right to Recall District Education Officer, RTR State Education Minister, RTR Central Education Minister , Saatya System etc etc.
.
So whatever public opinion we have generated on "no fail till class-8" may get hijacked, and worse, may also get wasted away as CoBhAp will create no solution to the mess.
.
Solution I propose is that activists should NOT get swayed away by "mere statements" of leaders and announcements. They focus on law-draft printed by Ministers and law-drafts they refused to print. And they should ONLY focus on private members' bills submitted by MPs and the private member's bill they refuse to submit.
.
I have listed law-drafts to reduce the mess in education in chap-30 of rahulmehta . com/301.htm and also in my facebook notes. Pls see my index notes, Pls search on word "education":

x = sqrt(i) + sqrt(-i) = ?? , here i = sqrt(-1)
.
PLS DO NO SEE ANSWER BELOW WITHOUT A TRY
.
.
.
.
.
.
.
.
.
.
.
.
.
.
.
.
.
.
.
.
.x^2 = i + -i + 2*sqrt(i)*sqrt(i) = 0 + 2 * sqrt(i * -i) = 2 !!
.
So x = sqrt(2), - sqrt(2) !! two answers
.

Politics of Congress, BJP and AAP - one and the same. AAP once protested against corruption and now AAP MPs refuse to submit private member's bills in Parliament to reduce corruption such as RTR-MLA, RTR-MP, RTR-CM, RTR-PM, RTR-judges, JurySys etc.

2-may-2014 ------ भाजपाईयों ने चीनी सामानों की होली जलाई
.
http://www.jagran.com/uttar-pradesh/ghaziabad-10354325.html
.
भाजपाईयों ने चीनी सामानों की होली जलाई
2-may-2014
.
जासंकें, गाजियाबाद : भारतीय सीमा पर चीन द्वारा किए गए अतिक्रमण के विरोध में बृहस्पतिवार को भाजपाईयों ने घंटाघर पर चीनी सामानों की होली जलाकर विरोध प्रदर्शन किया। भाजपाईयों ने चीन के विरोध में नारेबाजी की और लोगों से चीन निर्मित सामानों के बहिष्कार की अपील की।
.
इस मौके पर भाजपा के वरिष्ठ नेता अशोक गोयल ने कहा कि युवाओं को राष्ट्रसेवा में संगठित होकर चीन का मुंह तोड़ जवाब देना चाहिए। चीन की अर्थव्यवस्था को बढ़ावा देने में भारतीय बाजार का बहुत बड़ा योगदान है। चीनी सामान काफी संख्या में भारत में बिक रहे हैं। ऐसे में लोगों को चीनी सामान का बहिष्कार करना चाहिए।
.
भारतीय जनता युवा मोर्चा के संयुक्त क्षेत्र प्रभारी डा. विक्रम सिंह व ज्ञानेंद्र शर्मा ने कहा कि भारतीय सीमा में चीनी सैनिकों ने कब्जा जमा रखा है। लेकिन केंद्र सरकार मौन है। केंद्र सरकार की इस नकारात्मक रवैये से देश शर्मसार हो रहा है। विरोध प्रदर्शन में केके शुक्ला, ज्ञानेंद्र शर्मा, अरविंद अकेला, आशा शर्मा, इंदु जौहरी, नीरज चौधरी, सचिन सोनी, संजय कुशवाहा, अभिषेक सिंह, राजीव अग्रवाल, अशोक गोयल, राजेंद्र कुमार, एडवोकेट सुशील गौतम, मयंक गोयल, सौरभ जायसवाल शामिल थे।

comment-4 is by you
.
Then comment-3 and comment-4 are CONSECUTIVE comments and pls combine them
.
comment-1 and comment-3 are non-consecutive comments and so pls DO NOT combine them
.

.

Dear all,
.
Our organization ,"Right to Recall Group" is now registered under following name "Mahatma Sachindranath Sanyal Sanstha" . We didnt not get name "Right to Recall Group" as "Right to Recall" is name of the law in Madhya Pradesh etc. And so Charity Commissioner didnt approve the name "Right to Recall Group"
.
We do NOT have 80G or 35AC and nor we will apply for 80G or 35AC. Thats because I and most recallists believe that 80G and 35AC are thefts. Tax exemption is a theft. Our motto is "first pay taxes, and then contribute for your causes". So contribution to us will fetch you NO tax deductions.
.
Whatever contribution you send to us, 75% of it will be used for newspaper advt ONLY. And only the rest 25% will be used for other expenses.
.
PLEASE DO NOT SEND CONTRIBUTIONS IN DOLLARS OR CASH. Please dont make any cash deposit or dollar deposit. Whatever cash/dollar deposit you make will be deposited with Govt of India. We dont have FCRA license. So we cant take donations in dollars. Also, we are proposing that "no NGO should be allowed to take grants from foreigners". So if you wish to send donation etc from abroad, then pls first deposit the amount in your bank account in Indian bank branch located in India, and then send cheque or RTGS to us from that bank.
.
We dont want to take cash, as that would lower our credibility. So if you want to contribute small cash, please directly pay to a local recallist activist who is distributing pamphlets. If you wish to contribute cash for newspaper advt, then please deposit it in account of someone you know, and then please transfer into our account.
.
Bank - Union Bank of Inida
.

.

.
account type - savings account
.
Branch - S.M.E. JUNA VADAJ BRANCH
.

Thu Sep 18 2014 03:07:46 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Chinese soldiers party on Indian soil while "MMS" parties with Chinese PM
.
Dear NaMo-andhbhagats,
.
Please write one short essay. Say it is year 2013. And MMS is PM. And say Chinese soldiers are partying on Indian soil after harassing 100 Indian soldiers. And say MMS parties with Chinese PM in Delhi. Then what would you have written on FB? Would you have called for Facebook-bandh? Please write a 1000 word short essay.
.
=====
.
Chinese will invest $2,6 invest in Indigo. IOW, Chinese have will deposit $2.6 bllion with RBI, RBI will give Rs 2.6 * 6000 -crores = approx Rs 15600 crore to Chinese, and Chinese will invest that rupee in Indigo. And RBI has promised to repay dollars to Chinese for profits they earn and when they sell their capital. Now all sales of Indigo are in rupee. So Indigo will never be able to earn dollars. So liability of repaying dollars will pass on the head of GoI. Now where will GoI get dollars to repay investment and profits? From exports? Exports are rising at a rate slower than imports. And if dollars dont come, then Chinese can legally take away the temple gold deposited with SBI !!!
.
IOW, all this achcche din is FDI only. And FDI is like pole vaulting on thin ice.
.
Solution I propose is that we should focus on law-drafts that raise internal efficiency , such as Jury System, wealth tax, TCP, DDMRCAM, RTR, inheritance tax etc etc and not chase FDI.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Thu Sep 18 2014 09:59:21 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

FDI की सुनामी ले डूबी मोदी लहर को ।
___________________________
सरकार बनने के सिर्फ चार महीने बाद हुए उपचुनावों में मोदी लहर का मुलम्मा उतरता नज़र आ रहा है । नमो को नतीजे आने से पहले भनक भी नही लगी कि पेड मिडिया में धुँआधार प्रचार के बावजूद जमीन दरकने लगी थी ।
दिल्ली यूनिवर्सिटी के छात्र संघ के चुनावों की जीत का श्रेय भी नरेन्द्र मोदी को देने वाले उनके भक्त उपचुनावों में हुयी हार से नमो को उसी तरह बचा रहे है, जिस तरह गांधी परिवार के भक्त सोनिया-राहुल और प्रियंका को बचाते रहे है । जबकि नरेन्द्र मोदी और उनके डिप्टी शाह ने नतीजो पर चुप्पी साध रखी है ।
.
120 दिन के शासन काल में कुछ फैसले जिनका असर इस हार पर रहा :
1. नमो ने चुनाव विकास के मुद्दे पर भले ही लड़ा हो, लेकिन जो भारी वोट उन्हें आम चुनावों में मिला, उसका मुख्य कारण हिंदुत्व का अंडर करंट था । मंदिर, गाय, समान नागरिक संहिता जैसे मुद्दों पर चुप्पी साधने से समर्पित हिन्दू वादी कार्यकर्ताओं में भरोसा टूटा , मंदिरों के सोने की लूट ने बची खुची कसर पूरी कर दी ।
2. आम चुनाव के दौरान पेड मिडिया ने नमो के दर्ज़न भर इंटरव्यू का प्रसारण 24×7 किया था, जिस से सस्पेंडेड वोटर ने भावनात्मक लहर पर सवार होकर मतदान किया । चूंकि लहर कोई भी हो क्षणिक ही होती है, इसलिए ये मतदाता वर्ग फिर से उदासीन हो चुका था ।
3. नमो ने राष्ट्रवादी निति की जगह FDI जनित विकास की राह पकड़ी, इस से स्वदेशी को समर्पित कार्यकर्ताओं का नमो से मोह भंग हुआ, जबकि ग्रामीण और कृषक वर्ग अपने आप को बुलेट ट्रेन, स्मार्ट सिटी आदि के बेहिसाब प्रचार से खुद को अलग थलग समझने लगा ।
4. वरिष्ठ नेताओं को डाक में बिठाने, अन्य नेताओं के पर कतरने और पार्टी को हाई जेक करने से पार्टी के अन्दर मौन असंतोष पनपा और जनता में अक्खड़ मिजाज़ नेता की छवी बनी ।
5. सी- सेट पर सरकार ने ढुलमुल रवैया अपनाया ।
6. UP में मायावती ने उम्मीदवार न उतार कर और मुलायम ने आजम खान को पिंजड़े में डाल कर बीजेपी को भारी नुकसान पहुंचाया ।
7. RSS, SJM और BST के सत्ता में आते ही सुर बदलने को इन संघठनो के कार्यकर्ता पचा नहीं पाए ।
8. कोंग्रेस और अन्य पार्टी के नेताओं ने अंट शंट बकने और विवादित बयान देने से परहेज बरता ।
9. चूंकि उपचुनाव नेतृत्व परिवर्तन के लिए निर्णायक नही होते, इसलिए मतदाता उदासीन रवैया अपना लेते है ।
10. कार्यकर्ताओं के एक वर्ग विशेष को नमो से कुछ हिला देने वाले चमत्कार की उम्मीद थी, उनके हौसले टूट गए ।
.
बावजूद इसके नमो की FDI निति जारी है, चीन की सेना ने भारतीय सीमा में घुसपेठ कर के निर्माणधीन केनाल का कार्य रोक दिया है, उसी समय नमो चीनी पीएम ज़िनफिंग के साथ अहमदाबाद में नदी किनारे चहल कदमी कर रहे है ।
.
बदतर स्थिति यह है कि पेड मिडिया यह भ्रम फैला रहा है कि बीजेपी की हार इसलिए हुयी, क्योंकि नमो ने विकास को किनारे कर के योगी आदित्यनाथ के सहारे हिंदुत्व के मुद्दे पर वोट मांगे । जबकि सच्चाई इसके उलट है । योगी आदित्यनाथ ने लव जिहाद आदि पर बवाल सिर्फ UP में ही मचाया था, जबकि बीजेपी को सभी राज्यों में मुहं की खानी पड़ी । यहाँ तक की गुजरात में भी । स्पष्ट है कि ये जनादेश नमो के 120 दिन के कार्यकाल के आधार पर ही आया है । पेड मिडिया यह भ्रम फैला कर FDI की निति को जारी रखना चाहता है, जबकि अवाम ने FDI को सिरे से खारिज कर दिया है ।
.
कोंग्रेस और अन्य पार्टियाँ इसमें अपनी जीत देख रही है, लेकिन जनता 120 दिन पहले ही उनके कुशासन को खारिज कर चुकी है, और अब नमो से भी उम्मीदे टूटने के कगार पर है ।
तो फिर अब किसे चुनेंगे आप ? राहुल, मुलायम या नितीश को !!
मायने यह कि सत्ता या नेता परिवर्तन से देश के हालात में सुधार की गुंजाइश नही है ।
===
समाधान : कार्यकर्ताओं को यह समझना चाहिए कि नेता भक्ति और पार्टी पूजन से देश में वांछित वदलाव की उम्मीद करना बेमानी है, अत: कार्यकर्ताओं को राष्ट्र हित के कानूनों का प्रचार नागरिको में करना चाहिए, ताकि जन दबाव द्वारा जन हित के क़ानून पास करवाए जा सके ।

प्रजा अधीन राजा @ pk

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Fri Sep 19 2014 03:22:57 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

My conclusion after comparing may-2014 elections and recent sep-2014 byelections : in may-2014 , there was huge nagative vote against Congress, and so NaMo got tons of votes i.e. more than half the votes NaMo got were simply negative votes. The paidmedia showed that all negative votes as positive support. And these negative votes have still not become positive votes, despite so much paidmedia coverage.
.
MANINAGAR, AHMEDABAD, GUJARAT
----------------------------------------------------
.
Consider Maninagar Assembly Constituency , Ahmedabad, Gujarat. NaMo has been MLA from Maninagar since 2002. He has won three elections --- 2002, 2007, 2012 each with a huge landslide. In may-2014 Loksabha, Paresh Raval got huge surplus from Maninagar. This time also, in sep-2014 election BJP won.
.
But % polling in may-2014 was about 70% plus. And this time it was mere 36% !! In general, polling in sep is higher than may because may is hotter. This is trend across India , that polling in hotter month is lower, in general . But polling was half. IOW, large number of voters simply sat home. Their feeling was "I like party A. So I wont vote for B. But if A is losing, then let it lose, I dont want to help".
.
In may-2014, voting for BJP was must to stop Congress. So large number of anti-congress voters came out. But in sep-2014, their vote was neither going to change PM nor CM. So now only positive voters will show up. And voting % in Maninagar became half. This shows that positive voters of Congress as well as BJP are not even half of the total voters who vote for them. The paidmedia works round the clock to show that there is huge positive support for NaMo, by creating projection that all votes are positive votes, and by showing ethusiams of positive voters. But low polling in sep-2014 gives a less subjective proof.
.
AAP did NOT even field a candidate, despite the fact that they now have 100s of volunteers. This is because the national apex leadership doesnt want regional leaders to rise. Their volunteers are wasting time.
.
============
.
NINE CONSTITUENCIES IN GUJARAT - % voting fell significantly
-----------------------------------------------------
.
So out of 9 assembly seats which saw re-election in sep-2014, all 9 were previously held by BJP. And some 3 went to Congress and 6 went to BJP. And the % difference in votes has also reduced. And all constituencies except perhaps Matar, voting % fell compared to dec-2012 Gujarat assembly elections and also compared to may-2014 loksabha. So this also shows that both BJP and Congress have low positive voters..
.
AAP did NOT even field a candidate in any of the nine constituencies, despite the fact that they now have 100s of volunteers in each. This is because the national apex leadership doesnt want regional leaders to rise. Their volunteers are wasting time.
.
===============
.
Rest of the India .
---------------------
.
I have seen all details. But % polling has reduced in UP, Bihar etc. Total votes obtained by all parties went down.
.
The voting results in general election, when "who will be CM" is issue, will be much higher. And so BJP will be able to get more votes, as all those who want to block Akhilesh or Mayawati from becoming CM will now rush in to vote.
.
==========
.
So all in all, the shard decline in TOTAL polling % shows that no party has too many positive votes, and all parties are thriving on negative votes. AAP-apex doesnt want religion leaders to grow, and so doesnt even let them contest elections at their own money and time !!
.
As such, election outcomes no longer have much impact on future of people and nation. The future will depend on what law-drafts activists work to get passed. Elections as such will come and go.
.

----------------------------------------------------
.
Consider Maninagar Assembly Constituency , Ahmedabad, Gujarat. NaMo has been MLA from Maninagar since 2002. He has won three elections --- 2002, 2007, 2012 each with a huge landslide. In may-2014 Loksabha, Paresh Raval got huge surplus from Maninagar. This time also, in sep-2014 election BJP won.
.
But % polling in may-2014 was about 70% plus. And this time it was mere 36% !! In general, polling in sep is higher than may because may is hotter. This is trend across India , that polling in hotter month is lower, in general . But polling was half. IOW, large number of voters simply sat home. Their feeling was "I like party A. So I wont vote for B. But if A is losing, then let it lose, I dont want to help".
.
In may-2014, voting for BJP was must to stop Congress. So large number of anti-congress voters came out. But in sep-2014, their vote was neither going to change PM nor CM. So now only positive voters will show up. And voting % in Maninagar became half. This shows that positive voters of Congress as well as BJP are not even half of the total voters who vote for them. The paidmedia works round the clock to show that there is huge positive support for NaMo, by creating projection that all votes are positive votes, and by showing ethusiams of positive voters. But low polling in sep-2014 gives a less subjective proof.
.
AAP did NOT even field a candidate, despite the fact that they now have 100s of volunteers. This is because the national apex leadership doesnt want regional leaders to rise. Their volunteers are wasting time.
.
============
.
NINE CONSTITUENCIES IN GUJARAT - % voting fell significantly
-----------------------------------------------------
.
So out of 9 assembly seats which saw re-election in sep-2014, all 9 were previously held by BJP. And some 3 went to Congress and 6 went to BJP. And the % difference in votes has also reduced. And all constituencies except perhaps Matar, voting % fell compared to dec-2012 Gujarat assembly elections and also compared to may-2014 loksabha. So this also shows that both BJP and Congress have low positive voters..
.
AAP did NOT even field a candidate in any of the nine constituencies, despite the fact that they now have 100s of volunteers in each. This is because the national apex leadership doesnt want regional leaders to rise. Their volunteers are wasting time.
.
===============
.
Rest of the India .
---------------------
.
I have seen all details. But % polling has reduced in UP, Bihar etc. Total votes obtained by all parties went down.
.
The voting results in general election, when "who will be CM" is issue, will be much higher. And so BJP will be able to get more votes, as all those who want to block Akhilesh or Mayawati from becoming CM will now rush in to vote.
.
==========
.
So all in all, the shard decline in TOTAL polling % shows that no party has too many positive votes, and all parties are thriving on negative votes. AAP-apex doesnt want religion leaders to grow, and so doesnt even let them contest elections at their own money and time !!
.
As such, election outcomes no longer have much impact on future of people and nation. The future will depend on what law-drafts activists work to get passed. Elections as such will come and go.
.

ज्ञातव्य है कि चुनाव प्रचार में एक इंटरव्यू के दौरान अमेरिका द्वारा वीसा सम्बन्धी अनुमति न देने के बारे में पूछने पर मोदी ने कहा था कि जिसे आना हो भारत आएगा, हमे कहीं जाने कि जरुरत नही है ।

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Fri Sep 19 2014 13:14:27 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Dear, All,
.
This status is for those who want Right to Recall DPP = District Public Prosecutor law-draft. If you wish RTR over DPP, then please send following order to your MLA via SMS --- " Dear MLA, I order you to print http://tinyurl.com/RtrDPP draft in Gazette " .
.
One of the biggest reason for gohatya and rising organized crimes is because Public Prosecutors take bribes and weaken the case, RTR-DPP will reduce corruption in DPP and so gohatya and all other crimes will reduce.
.
Please post comments after reading first comments. Otherwise , comments posted will be deleted.
.
For Hindi translation, explanation etc. , pls see comments
.
====
.
Dear MLA,
.
If you have received the internet link to this status via SMS from your voter, then it is order to you, to order CM to print the following DRAFT in Gazette, The Hindi translation is given in the first and second comments.
.
===== start of the proposed RTR-DPP Gazette draft =======
.
The exact draft, which will come into effect when CM signs this law is as follows:
.
1. The word citizen would mean a registered voter in the district.
.
2. (procedure for Collector) If any citizen of India wishes to become DPP
= District Public Prosecutor , and he appears in person or via a lawyer with affidavit before the DC (or officer he deputes), the DC (or officer he deputes) would accept his application to become DPP after taking filing fee same as deposit amount for MP election.
.
3. (procedure for Talati or Talati’s clerks) If a citizen comes in person to Talati’s office, pays Rs 3 fee , and approves at most five persons for the DPP position, the Talati would enter his approvals in the computer and would him a receipt with his voter-id#, date/time and the persons he approved.
.
4. (procedure for Talati) The Talati will put the approvals of the citizen on district’s website with citizen’s voter-ID number and names of the persons he approved.
.
5. (procedure for Talati) If a the citizen comes to cancel his Approvals, the Talati will cancel one of more of his approvals without any fee.
.
6. (procedure for CM) Later, the CM may implement the above 3 procedures over SMS
.
7. (procedure for Collector) On every 5th of month, the Collector or officer he deputes will publish Approval counts for each candidate as on last date of the previous month.
.
8. (procedure for CM) If a candidate gets approval of over 35% of ALL citizens (ALL, not just those who have filed their approval) in a district, and it is 2% more than approval existing DPP has, then CM may appoint him as DPP
.
9 (procedure for CM ) As long as a DPP has approvals of more than 34% citizens , CM need not replace him. But if a DPP’s approval goes below 34%, the CM can replace him with the officer of his choice.
.
=============end of draft ===================

Dear MP,
.
Please order Railway Minister to issue print following orders in Gazette.
.
1. The Railways supervisor of VIP reservation will put names of all VIPs and how much reservations they used every in past 12 months monthwise and also annual totals. of past 5 years, on internet.
.
2. The Railways supervisor of VIP reservation will also setup system that each time a VIP reservation is used, that VIP would get an SMS and also an email. because that some VIP's signatures could be forged. So SMS and email will work as cross check.
.
3. The railways will charge Tatkal fare plus 25% extra on VIP reservations, except when VIP himself is personally travelling and is entitled for free journey OR VIP is Govt employee himself.

You have two options --- improve law-drafts , increase strength of policemen and wipe out Pindhara OR invite British. The British can finish Pindhara in weeks. So what would you as a king decide to do? You might be tempted to fix law-drafts of your land and increase internal strength of country and policemen. But then the local elitemen etc will dethrone you. So you might end up inviting British to destroy Pindhara. So NaMo's inviting FDI-mafia is similar step.
.
Solution I propose is that should inform voters and activists about the repatriation crisis FDI can create. ould oppose this inviting FDI-mafia and focus on law-draft to fix adminisration and raise internal efficiencies.

3. How SoMoKe's dependence of assorted land mafia, mineral mafia etc creates way for FDI-mafia
.
Now given these 10s of mafia prevailing in India, the industrial growth cant come. So one way is to take on this mafia, and other way is to invite FDI-mafia, The FDI-mafia are 10 times bigger than all mafia inside India, They can destroy all these nafia in a few months. But then, they will rule the roost.
.
An analogy is as follow. Say it is year 1800s and you are a king fed up with Pindhara. You have two options --- improve law-drafts , increase strength of policemen and wipe out Pindhara OR invite British. The British can finish Pindhara in weeks. So what would you as a king decide to do? You might be tempted to fix law-drafts of your land and increase internal strength of country and policemen. But then the local elitemen etc will dethrone you. So you might end up inviting British to destroy Pindhara. So NaMo's inviting FDI-mafia is similar step.
.
Solution I propose is that should inform voters and activists about the repatriation crisis FDI can create. ould oppose this inviting FDI-mafia and focus on law-draft to fix adminisration and raise internal efficiencies.

जनमत संग्रह में 55% स्कोटिश नागरिको ने ब्रिटेन के साथ बने रहने का फैसला किया है, लेकिन यह नतीजा भारत की मुश्किलें बढ़ा सकता है ।
___________________________
स्कॉटलेंड तक तो बात ठीक है, किन्तु कल को यदि लोकतंत्र के नाम पर UN ( USA-BRITAIN) जम्मू कश्मीर में जनमत संग्रह करवाने का दबाव डालता है तो :
1. सभ्य लोकतंत्र के तर्क के चलते हम जनमत संग्रह करवाने के दबाव में आ जायेंगे ।
2. यदि जनमत संग्रह होता है, तो हमे कश्मीर से हाथ धोना पड़ सकता है ।
3. हमारी सेना और अर्थव्यवस्था खुद के बूते इतनी मज़बूत नही है, कि हम कड़ा रुख अपना सके ।
4. हम हथियारों के लिए अमेरिका, ब्रिटेन और फ़्रांस (NATO) पर निर्भर है, ऐसे में जनमत संग्रह के लिए हम मजबूर किये जा सकते है ।
5. MNCs समर्थित आम आदमी पार्टी जितनी ज़ल्दी हो सके कश्मीर में जनमत संग्रह करवाने के पक्ष में है ।
6. धारा 370 को जारी रख के हमने स्वयं अपना पक्ष कमज़ोर कर रखा है ।
7. नमो बयान दे चुके है कि धारा 370 को ख़त्म नही किया जाना चाहिए, वरन इस पर बहस की जानी चाहिए ।
.
धारा 370 के चलते कश्मीर को विशेष राज्य का दर्ज़ा दिया गया है, अत: कश्मीर के संविधान को धारा 356 के तहत राष्ट्रपति द्वारा बर्खास्त नही किया जा सकता, न ही धारा 360 के तहत वित्तीय आपातकाल लगाया जा सकता है, वहीँ दोहरी नागरिकता के कारण पाकिस्तानी नागरिको को भी वहां की नागरिकता मिल जाती है ।
.
इस विषय पर विवाद नही है कि कोंग्रेस,सपा,बसपा और आम आदमी पार्टी का धारा 370 ख़त्म करने में इंटरेस्ट नही है । इनके नेता इस संबध में 60 साल से एक ही नारा लगा रहे है, कि कश्मीर भारत का अभिन्न अंग है । मतलब यह खुद भी मानते है कि अभिन्न अंग नही है, और अभिन्न अंग बनाने के लिए यह नारा काफी है ।
.
बीजेपी अपने एजेंडे में इस धारा को ख़त्म करने का वादा 1990 से ही करती रही है ।
वोट खींचू राजनेतिक बयानों को छोड़ दे, तो यह खुली हुई बात है कि बिना सेना को मज़बूत किये धारा 370 को ख़त्म नही किया जा सकता ।
तो नमो ने अपने 125 दिन के कार्यकाल में सेना को मज़बूत करने के लिए कितने क़ानून पास किये ?
: अंडा ।
.
हम 1947 से ही कश्मीर में लगातार जमीन खोते आये है, जबकि अब कश्मीर में 4 देश भारत, पाकिस्तान, चीन और अमेरिका फंसे पड़े है, अत: सेना मजबूत किये बिना कश्मीर को भारत का अभिन्न अंग नही बनाया जा सकता है ।

They have been working for IAC/AAP for now over 3.5 years. They worked for IAC/AAP because they want to reduce corruption.
.

But despite this, AK refuse.
.

.

Subramaniyam Swami ka Itihas >>> (Costing to Read about 10mints)
Subramanian Swamy was born at Mylapore, Chennai, India. His father Sitaram Subramanian is from Madurai, Tamil Nadu. In 1964, Swamy joined the faculty of economics at Harvard University (USA) beginning as an assistant professor in July 1966 and in 1969 became an associate professor.
.
He moved to the Indian Institute of Technology, Delhi and he was a professor of mathematical economics there from 1969 to 1991. He was removed from the position by its board of governors in the early 1970s but was legally reinstated in the late 1990s by the Supreme Court of India. Using IIT as his shield he supported Liberal economic policies but it didn't go well with the then Prime Minister Indira Gandhi who discredited him as 'Santa Claus with unrealistic ideas'. He was in favor of inviting more mor FDI FDI FDI. (liberal for FDI) This was the reason why he was removed by Indira Gandhi. But was reinstalled by Supreme Court. (Big Jacks)
.
During the period of emergency, he fled to the United States, seeking haven with an Indian businessman in Michigan who had become the spokesperson of opposition in the US. In 1976 when emergency was still in force and an arrest warrant issued in his name, he came to parliament to attend the session and managed to escape India after attending the session. This act made him a hero in the eyes of opposition parties. (Basically the then paid media supported him. Times of India is long since owned by Britishers since before independence till today)
.
Indira Gandhi invaded Bangladesh defeated and divided Pakistan. She had plans to invade Current Pakistanand divide it into 5 parts. Indira established RAW and did first Nuclear Test. This is made so controversial by Subramaniyam Swami that what is the truth is mostly polluted today. Anyways Swami then then joined JayPrakash Narayan (Kejriwal ka purvaj aka A. kejriwal of 1975) and then created Janta Party in 1977. JP was a Ramon Magsaysay Award (USA), winner1965 for Public Service.He opposed Indira Gandhi to his best. Indira Gandhi was successfully murdered by USA via transferring the security duty of the 2 Sikh guards who hated Indira Gandhi for her wrong approach of attacking Gurudvara. Indeed that was a big mistake of Indira Gandhi. However she was left with no choice as final solution to Bindrawala. This mistake made B.wala hero in Sikh community. Though many of them still dislike him as he chose Gurudvara to hide in and do politics, instead of dying in battle field.
.
Later Swami became president of Janta Party in 1990. During 1990 and 1991 Swamy was a member of the Planning Commission of India and Cabinet Minister of Commerce and Law of India. (In Congress Govt. which he pretends to hate) During this period he claims to have created the blueprint for economic reforms (New Economic Policy FDI FDI FDI loot country loot) (Media became more paid after this year when Door Darshan the govt channel eventually died) He in India under Prime Minister Chandra Shekhar which was later carried out in 1991 by Manmohan Singh, then Finance Minister under leadership of Prime Minister P. V.Narasimha Rao. (Congress Congress) In his book, Swami claims that Manmohan Singh has acknowledged it as well. (His works of Liberalization FDI Kranti)
.
In 1998 when Attalji did the Nuclear Test. He put court cases on Jay Lalita who had nirdaliya MPs supporting NDA Govt. He asked her to take the support back or else he will put her in jail forever. As a warning Jay Lalita was put into jail for 1 day and whole night. Attalji's Govt fell. Later there a sudden Airoplane hijack happened via Afghan terrorists (US supported)
US forced/helped Pakistan to invade Kargil region. Attalji had to face defeate. CTBT documents were long since created back then. Nuclear tests shut down. Attalji had to say sorry behind screen and had to sign papers of FDI in insurance companies. Swami enjoyed alot at that time.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Sep 22 2014 15:47:16 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Law-draft for narcotest in crimes like murder, rape, corruption, gohatya etc.
.
Dear Citizen,
.
If you want narcotest law for serious cases like murder, abduction, corruption, black money in swiss banks, gohatya then please send following order to MP via SMS ----
.
"Dear MP, I order you to print 'narcotest\brainmapping by Jurors in public over accused of murder, rape, high corruption, black money in swiss banks, gohatya etc' draft in Gazette. For full draft, pls see http://tinyurl.com/NarcoTest " .
.
(NOTE --- 1. please post comments after reading first comments. Otherwise , comments posted will be deleted. 2. To know about narcotest, please see comments below.)
.
Supreme judges have said that "narcotest of a person accused of murder, black money, corruption, rape, love jehad, gohatya , kidnapping, abduction, etc can be taken, but ONLY after accused approves !! This is stupid. This law-draft will enable Jurors to ask expert to conduct narcotest, brainmapping etc if and when needed.
.
Dear MP,
.
If you have recieved the url to this status via SMS , then please consider it as an order from the voter who has sent you the SMS. (not the author of this status). It refers to Jury System law-draft given at http://tinyurl.com/JurySysIndia
.
The SMS sender is ordering you to print the following law-draft in Gazette.

==== start of draft ====
.
1. This law-draft is to enable Jurors to order an expert to conduct polygraph or brainmapping or narcotest in private or in public. This draft will come in Gazette after Jury System draft has been printed in the Gazette.
.
2. (instruction for Jurors) With majority vote, the Jurors will decide if polygraph, brainmapping or narcotest should be conducted on the accused. A polygraph may or need not be conducted before brainmapping and a brainmapping may be or need not be conducted before narcotest.
.
3. (instruction for Jurors) The Jurors will decide if the test are to be conducted in private or public. If the name of a female victim has to be withheld, then the polygraph, brainmapping and narcotest must be done in private and Jurors cant make it public, Otherwise, it may be done in public
.
4. (instruction for JA = Jury Administrators) If number of Jurors are below 24, then JA will add Jurors using random selection method given in the Jury System draft, so that number of Jurors are 24.
.
5. (instruction for Jurors) If new Jurors are added, then each lawyer will speak for 30 minutes and may speak more if majority of new Jurors approve to inform about the case for at most 4 hours each. Plus, the Jurors will meet inside a room, away from public , for 1 hour to at most 12 hours, where old Jurors can inform new Jurors about the case.
.
6. (instruction for JA = Jury Administrator) After new Jurors are informed about the case, the JA using random lottery will divide Jurors into two equal groups Group-A and Group-B. If there are odd number of Jurors then the Group-B will have more 1 more Jurors. The Group-A will draft the questions to be asked and Group-B with majority decision will decide if question farmed should be asked.
.
7. (instruction for Jurors) If a person in Group-A wants to swap his position with a person in Group-B, then they may do so. The Jurors with majority approval, can also allow an expert in the panel of experts made by Grand Jurors to frame questions. The framing of questions will be done by Jurors in both Groups only, and will be done confidentially. The judge, lawyers etc cannot be there during their meetings
.
8. (instruction for Jurors) The question framing session will end when majority Jurors agree that question framing is over. The Jury Administrator will take the questions, and ask each Juror in Group-B whether he agrees with the question by only asking the question number, and not reading the whole question. Jury Administrator will give the list of questions approved by majority of Jurors in Group-B to the experts in-charge of polygraph, brainmapping and narcotests.
9 Jurors The Jurors with majority approval will decide which experts will conduct polygraph, brainmapping and narcotest. They will select the experts from panels approved by the Grand Jurors.
.
10. (instruction for Jurors) Based on answers given during the polygraph or brainmapping or narcotests, a Juror in Group-A or the expert can prepare a new question, and if majority in Group-B approve the question, then the expert will ask that question. The question will NOT be asked without approval of majority in Group-B
.
11. (instruction for Jurors) The Jurors will not take the statements made in all three tests as evidences
.
12. ( (instruction for Investigating Officer) The policemen investigating the case may gather more evidences based on the statements made during these tests
.
13. (instruction for Investigating Officer) With permission of Grand Jurors, the investigating officer may take polygraph or brainmapping or narcotest, before the Jury Trial begins. In such case, Grand Jurors may select 6 citizens at random and ask them to supervise the narcotest to ensure that no unrelated questions are asked by Investigating Officer.
.
14. Narcotest will not be taken without consent of Grand Jurors or Jurors. Even if accused consents, then also narcotest will require consent of Grand Jurors or Jurors.
.
CV.1 ( (instruction for DC) If a citizen wants to propose any change in this law, then the citizen can submit an affidavit demanding the change to District Collector or his clerk who will post it on the website of Prime Minister for a fee of Rs 20 per page.
.
CV.2 (instruction for Talati aka Patwari) If a citizen wants to register his opposition to this law-draft or any clause of this law-draft or wants to register any support to affidavit filed in the above clause, then he may register his YES/No for a Rs 3 fee at Patwari’s office. The Patwari will note the citizen’s YES/NO and will also post the citizen’s YES/NO on PM’s website.
.
Note ---- Please see CV.1 and CV.2 clauses at the end of the draft. Using these two clauses, the activists, if needed, can bring the changes in the narcotest procedures. Also, Jury administrator can use his discretion. And if he misuses the discretion, then the citizens can recall the District Jury Administrator. Thus there are sufficient checks and balances over experts in charge of narcotest and Jury Administrators.

===== end of draft =====

Read full post →

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Sep 23 2014 03:00:48 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How those who donate for cow are giving free food to cow meat eaters !! Solution : the donors should focus on law-drafts to imprison
.
Say a cow gives 300 kg of meat. Say price of grass is Rs 5 per kg and khod (wheat etc is Rs 15 per kg). A cow would need at least 3000 kg of khod and 9000 kg of wheat. So price will be 3000 * Rs 15 + 9000 * Rs 5 = Rs 45000 + Rs 45000 = Rs 90000 . If milk is taken, then more grass and more khod would be needed, but cost of that will come. So we are not counting it.
.
So Rs 90000 of khod and grass would be needed for 300 kg of meat. So meat would cost Rs 300 per kg plus profits.
.
But a donor comes, and donates money, and buys almost half the grass and khod for free. So now cost of meat reduces to Rs 150 or even less. Or putting other way, the cow donor gave half the meat to the cow meat eater for free.
.
This is the harsh reality !!
.
Solution is that those who are donating for cows should focus on law-drafts that would imprison cow slaughterers. If cow slaughter goes on, then donating for cow is just giving free cow meat to cow meat eaters.
.
Our sages instutionalized donation for cow as follows
.
1. cow milk is good for health of every man and thus community.
.
2. but cow milk is expensive compared to other milks, as cows give less milk
.
3. so rich donate for cow and thus subsidize NUTRITIOUS food for poor
.
4. so the sages institutionalized donations for cows.
.
All this is well, but cow slaughter creates a HUGE leakage in the system. If cow slaughter starts, then rich by donating for cow are only buying free cow meat for cow meat eaters !!
.
Solution is NOT to stop donations. But solution is that donors should spend money to publicize and bring law-drafts to reduce cow slaughter. Which law-drafts? Please see links in the second comment. Please send orders to MPs via SMS to get these law-drafts passed. Otherwise, by donating for cows, you will only give free food, strength and muscles to cow meat eaters.

Read full post →

3. so rich donate for cow and thus subsidize NUTRITIOUS food for poor
.
4. so the sages institutionalized donations for cows.
.
All this is well, but cow slaughter creates a HUGE leakage in the system. If cow slaughter starts, then rich by donating for cow are only buying free cow meat for cow meat eaters !!
.
Solution is NOT to stop donations. But solution is that donors should spend money to publicize and bring law-drafts to reduce cow slaughter. Which law-drafts? Please see links in the second comment. Please send orders to MPs via SMS to get these law-drafts passed. Otherwise, by donating for cows, you will only give free food, strength and muscles to cow meat eaters.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Sep 23 2014 03:44:50 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Right to Recall Doordarshan Chairman - to reduce political power of paidmedia
.
Dear Citizen,
.
Right to Recall Doordarshan will ensure that Doordarshan starts revealing all relevant truths So paidmedia channels such as paid-TimesNow, paid-NDTV etc etc will no longer get paid to suppress the truths. And with that, the money they have to fund all nude entertainment will reduce, In any case, the ability of paidmedia to influence politics will drastically reduce once right to recall doordarshan chairman comes.
.
(IMPORTANT NOTES --- 1. Please post comments after reading first comments. Otherwise , comments posted will be deleted. 2. For more on RTR-DD, please see second comment)
.

So If you, the voter if India, wish RTR-DD-Chairman law-draft to get printed in Gazette,. then please send following order to MP via SMS.
.
"Dear MP, please print Right to Recall Doordarshan Chairman draft in Gazette. Please see http://tinyurl.com/RtrDdChairman for full draft"
.
=====
.
Dear MP,
.
If you got the url to this status via SMS, then pls consider it as order from the voter who sent you the SMS. (not from the author of this status).
,
Please print Right to Recall Doordarshan Chairman law-draft in Gazette. The draft is as follows.
.
===== start of draft ==========
.
1. The word citizen would mean a registered voter
.
2. ( instruction to Cabinet Secretary ) .... If any citizen of India wishes to become DDC (Doordarshan Chairman) , and he appears in person or via a lawyer with affidavit before the Cabinet Secretary, the Secretary would accept his candidacy for DDC after taking filing fee same as deposit amount for MP election.
.
3. (instruction to Talati , or Talati’s Clerks) .... If a citizen of that district comes in person to Talati’s office, pays Rs 3 fee , and approves at most five persons for the DDC position, the Talati would enter his approvals in the computer and would him a receipt with his voter-id#, date/time and the persons he approved.
.
4. ( instruction to Talat ) ..... The Talati will put the preferences of the citizen on district’s website with citizen’s voter-ID number and his preferences.
.
5. (instruction to Talati) ...... If a the citizen comes to cancel his Approvals, the Talati will cancel one of more of his approvals without any fee.
.
6. (instruction to Cabinet Secretary) ...... On every 5th of month, the CS may publish Approval counts for each candidate as on last date of the previous month.
.
7. (instruction to PM ) ...... If a candidate gets approval of over 51% of ALL registered citizen-voters (ALL, not just those who have filed their approval) in a district, then PM may or need not expel the existing DDC and may or need not appoint the person with highest approval count as DDC. The decision of PM will be final.
.
TCP.1 ( instruction to District Collector ) ...... if any citizen wants a change in this law-draft, he may submit an affidavit at Collector’s office and Collector or his clerk will post the affidavit on the website of Prime Minister for a fee of Rs 20/- per page.
.
TCP.2 (instruction to Talati or Patwari) ..... If any citizens want to register his opposition to this law-draft or any section or wants to register YES-NO to any affidavit submitted in above clause, and he comes to Talati’s office with voter-ID and pays Rs 3 fee, Talati will enter YES/NO and give him a receipt. The YES-NO will be posted on the website of the Prime Minister.
.
===== end of draft ==========

Read full post →

Wed Sep 24 2014 14:28:00 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Exams are becoming easier and easier in Gujarat in maths\science 10th class, 12th class and engineering semesters !!
.
Some 90% questions in Maths in class-X and class-XII come from textbook only. Some 50% are solved examples given in textbooks. And in engineering, some 70% questions in finals come from past 5 years exams and solves examples of textbook.
.
Many students in engineering only read some 50 page sure suggestion booklet, and never even buy textbook and dont even touch reference books.
.
This is what I hear from a 3rd year engineering student.
.
Please write your FIRST hand experience only about Maths, science, engineering exams in Gujarat and other states. Pls no heresay.
.
Solutions I propoose are RTR District Education Officer, RTR State Education Minister, RTR National Education Minister, Saatya System, Jury System over Teachers in Govt schools etc etc. Please see https://www.facebook.com/notes/10150425669141922

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Wed Sep 24 2014 17:47:43 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

1. आखिर क्यों हम इतने कम बजट में मंगल यान सफलता पूर्वक भेज देते है, लेकिन एक अदद असाल्ट रायफल बनाने में हमें मुहं की खानी पड़ती है ?
2. इसरो ने साबित कर दिया है कि मोदी देश के नागरिको को गुमराह कर रहे है ।
___________________________

भारत विज्ञान और प्रोद्योगिकी के क्षेत्र में सदा से ही अगुआ रहा है, लेकिन विडम्बना है कि हमने प्रोद्योगिकी का उपयोग कभी हथियारों के आविष्कार और निर्माण में नहीं किया ।
नतीजे के रूप में हमारी प्रतिरक्षा प्रणाली कमज़ोर हुई, हमारी विरासत लूटी गयी, और आधुनिक हथियारों की तकनीक के अभाव में हमारी विश्व गुरुआयत और महानता का मान मर्दन हुआ ।
.
इतिहास में झाँक कर देखे तो मालूम होता है कि प्राचीन भारत में हमारे ऋषि मनीषियों ने आयुर्वेद, ज्योतिष, योग और धर्म की मीमांसा के साथ साथ हथियारों की तकनीक को भी महत्त्व दिया, अत: भारत महान के साथ साथ सुरक्षित और शक्तिशाली भी बना रहा । कालान्तर में हथियारों के निर्माण और अनुसन्धान में गिरावट आयी और हमारी महानता नारों में सिमट कर रह गयी ।

^ प्राचीन भारत में ऋषि मुनि आश्रम में हथियारों का अनुसन्धान करते थे, और योग्य शिष्यों को उन अमोघ अस्त्रों का दान देते थे ।
^ पुरोहितो और गुरुओ द्वारा विद्यार्थियों और नागरिको को हथियार चलाने का प्रशिक्षण दिया जाता था ।
कालान्तर में इस प्रवृति में बदलाव आने से हथियारों के अनुसंधान और निर्माण में गिरावट आयी, जिस से हम हथियारों की दौड़ में पिछड़ते चले गए ।
.
पिछले 2200 वर्ष में भारत पर कई विदेशी आक्रमण हुए और कमोबेश सभी में भारत की हार हुयी । सिकंदर से लेकर मुगलों तक, और ब्रिटिश से लेकर चीन तक भारत की पराजय का इतिहास बिखरा पड़ा है ।
आखिर भारत महान होने के बावजूद क्यों हारता चला आ रहा है ?
^ क्योंकि जब हम 60 किलो का 6 फूटी भाला ढो रहे थे तब, यूनान ने 18 फुट लम्बे भाले का आविष्कार कर लिया था ।
^ क्योंकि बाबर के पास ज्यादा उन्नत तोपखाना था ।
^ क्योंकि चंगेज़ खान ने भैस के सींगो और हिरन की आंतो के मिश्रण से घोड़े पर बैठ कर चलाये जा सकने वाले धनुष का आविष्कार कर लिया था ।
^ क्योंकि ब्रिटिश की तोपों की भीतरी सतह पर जर्मेनियम का लेप चढ़ा था, जिस से तोपों से लगातार गोले दागने के बावजूद बैरल गर्म नही होती थी ।
^ क्योंकि जब हम योग, आयुर्वेद, खगोल शास्त्र और ज्योतिष विज्ञान के अनुसन्धान में व्यस्त थे तब अन्य देश हथियारों की तकनीक पर काम कर रहे थे ।
नतीजा, जिस के मन आया उसने 10-20 हज़ार घोड़ो की टुकड़ी को भारत की तरफ हांक दिया, और हमें आधुनिक हथियारों के अभाव में अपनी जमीन और संपत्ति गंवानी पड़ी । यहाँ तक कि यही रवैया आज भी बदस्तूर जारी है ।
.
आजादी के समय भारत की सेना चीन से ज्यादा मजबूत थी, लेकिन जवाहर लाल गाज़ी ने 15 वर्ष पञ्चशील और गुटनिरपेक्ष के गीत गाने में गवां दिए जबकि इसी दौरान चीन अपनी सेना मज़बूत करता रहा ।
नतीजा हमें 1962 में मुहं की खानी पड़ी ।
.
शाष्त्री जी के संक्षिप्त कार्यकाल के बाद देवी इंदिरा गांधी का कार्यकाल हथियारों के निर्माण और प्रोद्योगिकी के लिहाज से स्वर्णयुग रहा, और भारत परमाणु शक्ति संपन्न देश बना । ज्ञातव्य है कि 1984 में ही द्वितीय परिक्षण की तैयारी पूर्ण हो चुकी थी, लेकिन देवी अम्मा के शहीद हो जाने से यह परिक्षण 1998 में सम्पन्न हो पाया ।
.
देवी इंदिरा अम्मा के बाद किसी भी प्रधानमन्त्री का सीना इतना चौड़ा और कंधे इतने मज़बूत नज़र नही आये जो विदेशी सत्ताओ से टकराव मोल लेकर भारत में स्वदेशी आधुनिक हथियारों के निर्माण के लिए आवश्यक क़ानून पास करने का साहस दिखाए ।
.
1990 से भारत की सेना के पाये लगातार कमजोर हो रहे है लेकिन पेड मिडिया, सत्ता लोलुप राजनेतिक दलों और RSS जैसे छद्म राष्ट्रवादी दलों द्वारा जानबूझकर इस संबध में नागरिको को जानकारी नही दी जा रही है कि :
^ आधुनिक लड़ाकू विमान, पनडुब्बी, युद्धपोत और मिसाइलो की तो बात ही छोड़ दीजिये, ज़मीनी लड़ाई के बुनियादी हथियार, असाल्ट रायफल की तकनीक तक पैदा करने में भारत नाकामयाब रहा है ।
^ यदि भारत को युद्ध का सामना करना पड़ता है, तो हमे सिर्फ विदेशी हथियारों का ही सहारा है ।
^ स्वदेशी निर्मित रायफल IN-SAS की हालत यह है कि ठन्डे इलाको में मैगजीन जाम हो जाती है, तथा गर्म प्रदेशो में ऑयल लीक होने लगता है ।
^ 2009 से स्वदेशी रायफल TAR के परिक्षण फेल हो रहे है और 5 वर्ष में भारत 5 रायफल बनाने तक में असफल रहा है ।
^ हमारे पास ऐसा कोई रडार नही है, जो चीन की क्रूज़ मिसाईल को डिटेक्ट कर सके ।
^ यह कि हमारे फौजियों को पानी की बोतल कारतूस की पेटी में डालकर ले जानी पड़ती है क्योंकि वेस्ट बेल्ट में बोतल टांकने का हुक नहीं है, कि फौजियो के किट में टोर्च और चाकू तक शामिल नही है, कि वेस्ट पाउच घटिया और बजने वाले प्लास्टिक के बने है, जो कि रात में आवाज करते है, कि घटिया क्वालिटी के चलते फौजियों को जूते और हेलमेट तक बाज़ार से खरीदने पड़ते है ।
^ यह कि यदि हमारी सेना रुपी दीवार टूट जाती है, तो नागरिको के पास लड़ने के लिए सिर्फ सब्जी काटने के चाकू और नेल कटर है ।.
हथियारों के निर्माण के लिए कई देशो के प्रतिरोध का सामना करना पड़ता है । इसी से 1998 के असफल परिक्षण के बावजूद अमेरिका ने भारत पर दबाव डाल कर 123 ट्रिटी पर हस्ताक्षर करवाए, ताकि भारत भविष्य में कभी परमाणु परिक्षण न कर सके । मंगल आदि के अभियानों से दुश्मन देश को कोई सामरिक खतरा नहीं होता, इसलिए ऐसे अभियानों के संचालन निर्बाध होते है ।
===
2. तब : नमो ने जब बयान दिया था कि 'भारत में तकनीक पैदा करने की काबलियत नहीं है, अत: हमें रक्षा क्षेत्र में तकनीक का आयात करना होगा' तब उनके कंधे झुके हुए थे तथा आवाज में गर्मजोशी नही थी ।
अब : आज नमो आत्मविश्वास से लबरेज नज़र आये, जब वो भारतीय वैज्ञानिकों के मंगल मिशन का गुणगान कर रहे थे । उनकी आँखे चमक रही थी और हाथ उसी तरह लहरा रहे थे जिस तरह चुनाव प्रचार के दौरान लहराया करते थे । यह आत्मविश्वास स्वदेशी उपलब्धि की देन है ।
.
इसरो के वैज्ञानिको ने साबित किया है कि भारत में उच्च तकनीक को अपने बूते पैदा करने की कूव्वत है, फिर हम अपने रक्षा प्रतिष्ठान विदेशियों के हवाले क्यों कर रहे है ?
जब हम मंगल यान बना सकते है, तो हथियार क्यों नही ?
पिछले ढाई दशक से स्वदेशी हथियारों के निर्माण के बेस को सभी पार्टियों ने बुरी तरह से तोड़ा है, जबकि नमो से उम्मीद थी कि वे रक्षा में स्वदेशी तकनीक के निर्माण को प्रशस्त करने वाले कानूनों को पास करेंगे, वे रक्षा के क्षेत्र में भारत को हमेशा के लिए परजीवी बनाने पर आमादा है ।
.
अभिनन्दन वक्तृता में नमो ने कहा कि मंगल अभियान से कुछ देशो को इर्ष्या हो सकती है, उनका इशारा चीन की तरफ है, लेकिन उन्हें यह भी ध्यान रखना चाहिए कि चीन अपने लड़ाकू विमान, लेसर गाइडेड मिसाइल और क्रूज़ मिसाइलो का निर्माण स्वयं करता है,जो कि युद्ध के दौरान ज्यादा उपयोगी साबित होंगे ।
---
समाधान : इस प्रकार के अभियानो से संचार तकनीक का विकास होता है, जो कि रक्षा क्षेत्र के जटिल संचार उपकरणों के विकास में सहायक हो सकते है । हमें जरूरत है ऐसे कानूनों को पास करने कि जिस से हम इस तकनीक प्रयोग हथियारों के निर्माण में कर सके ।
नागरिको से निवेदन है, अपने क्षेत्र के सांसद को SMS द्वारा ऑर्डर भेजे कि ज्यूरी ट्रायल, सात्य प्रणाली, WOIC, राईट टू रिकाल पीएम तथा रक्षा मंत्री के कानूनों को गेजेट में छापा जाए, तथा रक्षा क्षेत्र में FDI की अनुमति नही दी जाए ।
इन कानूनों के ड्राफ्ट यहाँ से मुफ्त डाउनलोड करे :
rahulmehta . com / 301 . h. htm
*मंगल अभियान की सफलता पर इसरो के वैज्ञानिकों तथा उन सभी तकनीशियनो को बधाई, जिनका इसमें सहयोग रहा ।

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Sep 25 2014 10:54:32 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Please see Make In India website for yourself , and please see what a sick joke it is.
.
Please click "Recent Policy Measures" at makeinindia. com/policy/foreign-direct-investment/ (see link in first comment ) .
.
It says
.
RECENT POLICY MEASURES
.
100% FDI allowed in the telecom sector.
.
100% FDI in single-brand retail.
.
FDI in commodity exchanges, stock exchanges & depositories, power exchanges, petroleum refining by PSUs, courier services under the government route has now been brought under the automatic route.
.
Removal of restriction in tea plantation sector.
.
FDI limit raised to 74% in credit information & 100% in asset reconstruction companies.
.
FDI limit of 26% in defence sector raised to 49% under Government approval route. Foreign Portfolio Investment up to 24% permitted under automatic route. FDI beyond 49% is also allowed on a case to case basis with the approval of Cabinet Committee on Security.
.
Construction, operation and maintenance of specified activities of Railway sector opened to 100% foreign direct investment under automatic route.
.
==============
.
So a manufacturing unit which is 100% owned by Chinese, using 100% made in China machines, using apex Management and apex technical staf of China, using low rung tehnicians and cleaners of India , will make goods. And that will qualify as "Make in India" !! This is really really sick joke and nothing else.
.
All in all, NaMo is just implement MMS's 1991 FDI policies more "efficiently" and nothing more using Swadeshi labels like "Make in India". And at the same time, he refuses to increase courts, fix tax laws etc for local manufacture. eg consider China, It has 200,000 courts while India has 20000. And China uses merit system for recruitment in judges while India uses age old "recommendation and hereditary" system for recruitment in judiciary. And China is now also moving towards Jury System. End result is --- Chinese manufacturer get far far LESS UNFAIR courts than Indian manufacturers. Then China has lower land prices, cheaper electricity and so forth. HowTH will Indian compete with Chinese given that NaMo refuses to increase number of courts, refuses to install Jury System and opposes merit based recruitment in judges. So Indian factory owners will simply get slaughtered by Chinese. And also by Japanese , American etc.
.
And finally, Americans will have highest upper hand in FDI, as they have the most dollars and most oil wells, their own or looted. So with all this FDI, NaMo will prove as far more pro-American PM. All this anti-American posture is only to please those who still like US-bashing.
.
Solution I propose is REAL "make in india", i.e. "made in India, made by Indians" . For that we need to fix reduce corruption in courts, police etc, fix labor/tax laws and many more things. For this we need to print law-drafts such as TCP, RTR, Jury, wealth tax, DDMRCAM etc. And for that, we need to send orders to MPs via SMS to get those DRAFTS printed in the Gazette. To see list of orders we need to send to MPs, via SMS, please see tinyurl .com/RahulMehtaSms

This is what happens when a leader start attending YMCA meetings.

NaMo indirectly takes potshot at Asaram Bapu . paid-Indian-Express quotes as NaMo's exact words as "I have to say to all the male members that women are never at fault. .... ". What does it mean to say that "women are never at fault"? This means that "NO women will ever make false allegation" . Which means that allegation made on Asaram is correct, even without any inspection or investigation. Hell, I see 100s of take allegations in 498A cases, and just few days back, I read news that a woman had made 22 separate rape complains, on separate unrelated accused, on separated dates spanning over 3 year period !! Surely, as per NaMo, even she cannot be at fault !!

paid-Hindu-newspaper says that "Mr. Modi is learnt to have expressed his displeasure at the BJP leaders from Madhya Pradesh defending Asaram." and paid-Hindu-newspaper also says "Modi is learnt to have asked BJP leaders to refrain from making statements in Asaram's support. " . I dont know if paid-Hindu-newspaper should be trusted on this.

All in all, NaMo has taken pot-shots at Asaram Bapu, clearly indicating that which side is he on.

Now I am anti-Asaramp-Bapu , but thats because he practices Tantrik and he is land encroacher. Solution I propose is to enact law-drafts that would improve lives of people and reduce demand of Tantrik. And we should make ALL land ownership records public and land disputes Jury based so that encroachments becomes easy to prove and possible to punish. But allegations of nar-bali (human sacrifice) and child-molestations seem to be cooked up. As far as this specific case goes, it, one has to take stand and so I will indeed take stand. I support Jury Trial and narco-test in public on Asaram Bapu, and if Jury finds allegations baseless, then Jury should be empowered to do narco-test in public on girls' father to see if allegations were correct or deliberately cooked. And if allegations seem right, then Jury can imprison him for 7 years. The Jury should be chosen from National Voter list and should have 500 members. Is Jury of 500 impossible? well, Greeks in 500 BC used to have Jury of 500, 1000 and even 1500 !! So surely, with far netter technology we have today compared to Greek ofs of 600 BC, Jury of 500 is very much possible. Jury and narco-tests will dig out all the truth we need. But I request reader to note that NaMo is not demanding any Jury Trial or narco-test in public. He is simply siding with corrupt Rajasthan IPS and corrupt Rajasthan judges, despite the very possibility that they may have cooked up out rightly false allegations.

As examples of false allegations (a) Jayalalitha, another Missionary agent, wanted plot from Shankracharya for cheap. He refused, and so Jayalalitha threw false murder allegation on him. (b) Sonia Ghandy asked Gautam Adani not to place high bid for Ahmedabad Team in IPL as that would have forced Sonia-agent Shashi Tharoor to raise the bids. Gautam Adani didnt agree and so CBI arrested his brother in 2 bit customs case !!!

In Asaram case too, there was a rumor that land encroach Robert Vadhera wanted to buy a plot from Asaram Bapu for dirt cheap , and Asaram denied. All in all, India has history of Ministers, IPS, IAS etc cooking false cases and judges collaborating with it. So we MUST demand a Jury Trial in this case.

But thats not the stand naMo has taken. He has silently agreed not to give any assistance to enemies of Asaram, be it is Sonia or Robert Vadhera or Missionaries or all 3 combined .

====

In may-2013, NaMo was chief guest at annual all India YMCA function as Ahmadabad. YMCA is an organization whose official goal as officially stated on is website and wikipedia-page, is to "spread message of Jesus to every youngman and young woman in the world". If a leader is becoming Chief Guest at such function, one should understand whose side he will take when fight between Missionaries and Hindu sages break out.

And if allegations seem right, then Jury can imprison him for 7 years. The Jury should be chosen from National Voter list and should have 500 members. Is Jury of 500 impossible? well, Greeks in 500 BC used to have Jury of 500, 1000 and even 1500 !! So surely, with far netter technology we have today compared to Greek ofs of 600 BC, Jury of 500 is very much possible. Jury and narco-tests will dig out all the truth we need. .... As examples of false allegations (a) Jayalalitha, another Missionary agent, wanted plot from Shankracharya for cheap. He refused, and so Jayalalitha threw false murder allegation on him. (b) Sonia Ghandy asked Gautam Adani not to place high bid for Ahmedabad Team in IPL as that would have forced Sonia-agent Shashi Tharoor to raise the bids. Gautam Adani didnt agree and so CBI arrested his brother in 2 bit customs case !!!
.
In Asaram case too, there was a rumor that land encroach Robert Vadhera wanted to buy a plot from Asaram Bapu for dirt cheap , and Asaram denied. All in all, India has history of Ministers, IPS, IAS etc cooking false cases and judges collaborating with it. So we MUST demand a Jury Trial in this case.
But thats not the stand naMo has taken. He has silently agreed not to give any assistance to enemies of Asaram, be it is Sonia or Robert Vadhera or Missionaries or all 3 combined .
.

.
Pls read ull post at fb. com/mehtarahulc/posts/10151573137886922
.
======
.
Why Jury Trial and NOT judge trial? The judges have nexuses with lawyers, Ministers and what not. Also, the lower court judfes always feel threatebed by High and Supreme judges as High and Supreme judges can anyday expel/suspend/transfer them. And the lower court judges also get swayed by high/supreme judges promises of promotion and favorable transfer. so lower courts judges are unrelaible.
.
The High and supreme judges' sons and close relatives get crores of rupees worth fees and so high and supreme judges too are highly compromised. Further, these days, the biggest fee payers are foreign companies and many judges sons work as lawyers for foreign company owners. And so judges cannot be and should not be trusted. And IMO, most citizens no longer have faith in high\supreme judges
.
My proposal to enact Jury System and hand over the case to Jurors. The Jurors have no aprior nexuses. And the trials end in a few days, as one Jury gets ONLY one case and hearing are from 10 am to 5pm everyday. And the Jurors have no gear of transfer, suspension, expulsion etc and no greed of promotion. So they can decide the case better than a judge can.
.
And this is my proposal for ALL 3 crore pending cases in India, that all cases should be given to Juries and not corrupt/nexused/nepotic judges. And this is something I have been proposing demanding since 1998 for all cases !!
.
So once again, I never said that "Sant Asaram Bapu and Narayan Swamy should be acquitted without trial". But I have no faith in judges and so trial in both cases, and ALL 3 crore cases, should be by Jurors and Jurors only. And judges should be recallable , the way judges in USA and Japan are recallable by citizens.
.
Solution I propose is ---- those who want Jury System in India, and Right to recall judges law in India, should order MPs via SMS to print Jury System law-draft and RTR judge law-draft in Gazette, For the SMS text, please see inyurl .com/JurySysIndia and tinyurl .com/RtrDistJudge . Or please see link in first comment

घटनाक्रम कहता है कि उद्धव के साथ वही खेल हो रहा है, जो नितीश के साथ हुआ था ।
नितीश की तरह उद्धव की जबान भी नमो के खिलाफ कैंची की तरह चलती रही है, और विरोध के तेवर मोदी को बरदाश्त नही है ।
नितीश ने दबाव बनाने की कोशिश की थी, नमो ने समझौता वार्ता तक करना मुनासिब नही समझा, इसी तर्ज़ पे शाह ने तीन बार महाराष्ट्र दौरा टाल दिया ।
मोदी के वर्चस्व को चुनौती देने से पहले उद्धव को केशु भाई, वघेला, संजय जोशी, आडवाणी, स्वराज, जसवंत, मुरली मनोहर और नितीश की दुर्दशा से सबक लेना चाहिए ।
.
बहरहाल राज ठाकरे के चन्द्रमा बली चल रहे है, किन्तु सेना और बीजेपी का गठबंधन नही टूटता है तो शरद पवार कहीं के नही रहेंगे ।

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Fri Sep 26 2014 20:46:06 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

'MADE vs MAKE in India'
.
MADE IN INDIA जहाँ देश में स्वदेशी तकनीक का विकास करके भारत को आत्मनिर्भर बनाएगा, वहीँ MAKE IN INDIA से हम तकनीक के क्षेत्र में पंगु हो जायेंगे,हमारी स्वदेशी इकाईया बंद होगी, प्राकृतिक संसाधन नीलाम होंगे, और भारत फिर से विदेशी सत्ताओ का उपनिवेश बन जाएगा ।
.
¶ तो क्या चुनेगें आप ???
___________________________

कृपया Make in india की वेबसाईट देखें, कि किस तरह इस नारे की आड़ में देश का चप्पा चप्पा विदेशियों को बेचा जा रहा है ।
कुछ बानगी :
: टेलीकॉम क्षेत्र में 100% विदेशी निवेश ।
: सिंगल ब्रांड रिटेल में 100% विदेशी निवेश ।
: रक्षा में विदेशी निवेश की सीमा 26% से बढ़ाकर 49% की गयी । यह सीमा विशेष मामलो में केबिनेट कमिटी की अनुमति से और भी बढ़ाई जा सकेगी ।
: बीमा क्षेत्र में विदेशी निवेश की सीमा 26% से बढ़ाकर 49% की गयी ।
: चाय प्लांट्स में विदेशी निवेश के प्रतिबंध को हटाया गया ।
: क्रेडिट इन्फोर्मेशन क्षेत्र में 74%, तथा पूंजीगत पुनर्निर्माण के क्षेत्र में 100% विदेशी निवेश को अनुमति ।
: रेलवे क्षेत्र में निर्माण, संचालन, अनुरक्षण तथा अन्य सम्बंधित गतिविधियों में 100% विदेशी निवेश की अनुमति ।
: कमोडिटी, स्टॉक, पॉवर एक्सचेंज, पेट्रोल शोधन और कूरियर सर्विस के क्षेत्र में ऑटोमेटिक रूट से विदेशी निवेश की अनुमति ।
.
तो अब चायनीज़ यहाँ जो फेक्ट्रीयां लगायेंगे उनमे चीन की आधुनिक आयातित मशीने होंगी, चायनीज़ इंजिनियर और चायनीज़ प्रबंधक होंगे । भारतीयों के हिस्से इन फेक्ट्रीयों में चतुर्थ और तृतीय श्रेणी के गैर तकनिकी कार्य आने वाले है ।
.
चीन में स्वदेशी उद्योगों को कानूनों द्वारा सरंक्षित किया गया है, जबकि नमो ने भारत की स्वदेशी इकाइयों को सरंक्षण देने वाले कानूनों को पास करने से इनकार कर दिया है ।
ज्ञातव्य है कि अमेरिका में स्वदेशी इकाइयों को सरंक्षण देने के लिए जूरी सिस्टम है, जापान में आंशिक रूप से न्यायपालिका को प्रजा अधीन किया गया है, जबकि अब चीन भी न्यायतंत्र को प्रजा अधीन करने की दिशा में बढ़ रहा है, तब भारत भ्रष्ट और सुस्त जज सिस्टम से चिपका हुआ है ।
.
चीन में मुकदमो के तेजी से निपटान के लिए 2 लाख जज है, जबकि भारत में सिर्फ 20 हज़ार ।
चीन में निष्पक्ष जजों की नियुक्ति में लिखित परीक्षा को आधार बनाया गया है, जबकि भारत आज भी पाषाण युगीन साक्षात्कार और अनुशंषा आधारित नियुक्तियों को लागू किये हुए है, फलस्वरूप भारत की न्याय प्रणाली दिनों दिन सुस्त और भ्रष्ट होती जा रही है ।
यही नही, नमो ने स्वदेशी उद्योगों को बचाने के लिए प्रोविडेंट फंड, लेबर लॉ, उत्पाद शुल्क और पर्यावरण सम्बन्धी कानूनों में सुधार करने से भी इनकार कर दिया है, जबकि MNCs को आयकर और अन्य करो में छूट ज़ारी है ।
.
ऐसे में यह तय है कि चायनीज़ हमारी पहले से बदहाल स्वदेशी इकाइयों को निर्ममता से हलाल कर देंगे और बचे खुचे उद्योगों को अमेरिकन कम्पनियां निगल जायेंगी । जितना ही ये MNCs रूपये में मुनाफा कमाएगी हमें उसका भुगतान डॉलर में करना होगा, हम पहले ही डॉलर के क़र्ज़ में गले गले तक डूब चूके है, ऐसे में ये FDI की सुनामी भारत को तहस नहस कर देगी ।
.
भारत में हर कोई जानता है कि MADE in india के मायने होते है, स्वदेशी उद्योगों को बढ़ावा देना,स्वदेशी तकनीको का आविष्कार करना तथा स्वदेशी संसाधनों की सहायता से स्वयं के बूते उत्पादन करना, ताकि देश आत्मनिर्भर बन सके ।
लेकिन नमो ने सदियों से चले आ रहे इस सिद्धांत को ही पलट दिया है, वे अपने झोले में से एक नया सिद्धांत MAKE in india निकाल लाये है ।
.
आज के 400 वर्ष पहले भारतीय राजाओं ने प्रत्यक्ष विदेशी निवेश को अनुमति दी थी, नतीजे में हमने 200 वर्ष की गुलामी भुगती ।
तब यदि मोदी साहब जैसे मार्केटिंग के कलावंत होते तो, इसे विकास के लिए MAKE in india का नाम देते । आखिर ईस्ट इंडिया कम्पनी ने देश में रेल की पटरियां बिछाई, टेलीग्राफ ले आये और न जाने कितना विकास किया, वे भी भारत को तकनीक ही देने आये थे ।
शायद हमें उन्हें रोक लेना चाहिए था ।
.
अब नमो विदेशी उद्योगपतियों को ढोल बजाकर कह रहे है, कि आप तकनीक लेकर आओ और भारत का विकास करो । शायद भारत इसीलिए पिछड़ गया था, क्योंकि तब विकास की जिम्मेदारी सिर्फ एक कम्पनी पर थी, लेकिन अब विकास की गति 3000 गुना ज्यादा रहने वाली है । मतलब जो फैसले मनमोहन ने सिर झुका कर चुपचाप किये, उन्हें मोदी साहब पूरी दबंगई से कर रहे है ।
मन को इसके लिए गालियाँ मिली थी, जबकि नमो को तालियाँ मिल रही है ।
निदा फाजली कहते है :
सब के जैसे होकर भी, बाइज्ज़त है बस्ती में,
कुछ है लोगो का सीधापन, कुछ है अपनी अय्यारी भी ।
नमो भी पहुंचेले अय्यार है । उन्होंने सिर्फ made को make कहा, FDI को First Devlope India कहा, और जनता को विश्वास दिला दिया कि यह UPA -3 नही है ।
.
असल में FDI के सियार को MAKE in india की नील से रंगा जा रहा है । इस से देश में MNCs को भारत में पाँव जमाने का और भी अच्छा अवसर मिलेगा ।
यह अनुभव सिद्ध है कि MNCs के आने से हमारी स्वदेशी इकाइयां टेक ओवर या बंद हो जायेगी, गणित विज्ञान की शिक्षा टूटने से हम तकनीकी रूप से परजीवी हो जायेंगे, इस से हमारा पहले से गिरा हुआ निर्यात और गिरेगा, हम डॉलर संकट में और भी गहरे फंस जायेंगे, डॉलर ख़त्म होने और सोना लुटने के बाद हमारे खनिज और राष्ट्रीय संपदाएँ नीलाम हो जायेंगी, MNCs के बढ़ते मुनाफे से SCR में वृद्धि होगी, फलस्वरूप बड़े पैमाने पर धर्मान्तरण होंगे और हम फिर से एक औपनिवेशिक राष्ट्र बन जायेंगे ।

इसी सर्कस को नमो द्वारा MAKE in india कहा जा रहा है, जो कि देश के लिए दु:स्वप्न साबित होगा ।
===

इन सभी कानूनों के ड्राफ्ट्स यहाँ से मुफ्त डाउनलोड करे :
rahulmehta . com / 301 . h . htm

comment-2 is by me

.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Sep 27 2014 07:15:20 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

FDI means 'fully destroy independence". Calling it "first develop India" is a linguistic fraud.
.
Consider 22-sep-1857 . It was the day when British troops defeated Indian soldiers in Delhi and British soldiers took over Delhi. This day is marked as end of 1857-revolution and also an end of Mughal tyranny. Now consider calling it as "independence day" !! well, that would be nothing but a linguistic acrobat or linguistic fraud.
.
Same way FDI aka Foreign Direct Investment means 'fully dependent India' or rather ' fully destroy independence'. Calling FDI as 'first develop India' and then calling it as integral part of 'make in India' are linguistic frauds. And given that these linguistic frauds are coming from none but PM himself, they are linguistic frauds of highest proportion.
.
And it is sad to see that each and every NaMo-andhbhagats, and BJP-RSS-BST-worker referring FDI as 'first develop India' !!
.
The FDI-spree should be seen with the FACT that "120 days arent enough" excuse is thrown like a rubber stamp everywhere when one asks to print law-drafts to reduce inefficiency of local manufacturers..
.
1. local manufactures want number of courts to increase from 20000 to 200000 so that dispute resolution can speed up. Reply? 120 days arent enough
.
2. local manufactures want law-drafts to simply tax code and get away with corruption prone law-drafts like excise, VAT, GST, service tax etc Reply? 120 days arent enough
.
3. Local manufacturers want law-drafts to reduce corruption in supreme\high\lower courts and also admin. Reply? 120 days arent enough
.
But looks like 120 days are enough to print law-drafts to enable foreigners to do what they wanted !!
.
Well, lets do a reality check. 120 days may not be actually have 200,000 courts, But 12 days are enough to allocate funds, and create vacancies, setup guidelines for written tests to recruit staff, and issue order for written tests. Likewise, 12 days are enough to print Citizens' Review over Judiciary drafts --- the kind of drafts Japan and USA have. And 12 days are enough fix much of the tax code --- details may take time. But none of this happened in 120 days. But in same 120 days, all changes that owners of foreign companies wanted did come.
.
IOW, what we are seeing is rampant use "120 days arent enough" excuse to oppose law-drafts needed to reduce inefficiencies of local factory owners. And foreigners is welcomed under "make in India" banners and "foreigners-Indian bhai bhai" love fest,
.
So the local manufacturers cant become efficient and can survive against MNC-owners who have faster and less corrupt courts\admin in West. So local factory owners will become all satellites to MNC-owners. Some may be large satellites, and some may be small satellites. But they will be satellites. And once MNC-owners see no need to pamper India, they will just wipe it clean, the way they wiped out Iraq and Libya.
.
Solution I propose is that activists should STOP neta-bhakti such as Sonia bhakti, NaMo-bhakti, AK-bhakti etc. And they should focus on law-drafts and work on 'order your MP via SMS' campaign to bring this law-drafts. The law-drafts I propose are TCP, DDMRCAM, JurySys, RTR, wealth tax etc etc. . And orders you can send to MPs via SMS is listed in tinyurl. com/RahulMehtaSms
. Otherwise, verbal acrobats like FDI = First Develop India or FDI = Foreigners will Develop India etc are timepass. And we dont have infinite time to pass

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Sep 28 2014 03:52:52 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Order your MP via SMS to bring proposed\demanded Central Govt Gazette Notification to fix incompleteness in POCSO to deteremine age of victim.
.
Dear citizen,
.
This status post is NOT specific to Sant Asaramp Bapu. It is for 100s and perhaps 1000s of persons who are being rightly or wrongly prosecuted under POCSO. The post does not say that "Sant Sri Asaram Bapu must be acquitted etc etc". The post is ONLY about POCSO law.
.
(Post comments are reading first comments. Otherwise, comments will be deleted. Additional links are in 1st comment)
.
POCSO says that if victim is below 18 years, then punishment on accused , if proven guilty, will be much higher, That is aside. But POCSO doesnt give guidelines to decide age of victim. So many people are able to misuse POCSO using dubious age certificates.
..
POCSO section-46 allows Central Govt aka PM to issue notification to address incompleteness with the act.
.
So if you citizen wish to address this problem then Irequest you to send following order to MP via SMS .
.
"Dear MP, I a voter of your constituency order you to order PM to print a Gazette Notification to reduce incompleteness in deciding age in POCSO . The draft of GN is given at http://tinyurl.com/PocsoAge ".
.
======
.
DEAR MP,
.
If you got the url to this status via SMS, then pls consider it as order from the voter who sent you the SMS. (not from the author of this status).
,
Please order PM to print following Gazette Notification.
.
=== start of proposed GN====
.
1. This GN is printed under POCSO Act, section-46(1).
.
2. This GN addresses the incompleteness about deciding age of victim in the POCSO Act. And this GN is to be applies when there are two of more age certificates to decide the age of the victim.
.
3. In case of there are two or more age certificates to decide age of victim, and all valid age certificates came into effect or existence more than 3 years before the crime happened, then the age certificate which shows higher age will be taken to decide the age of victim,.Also, if the older age certificate shows higher age, then order age certificate will be taken.
.
4, If age certificate is illegal or issued thru forged means, then it would not be taken into account
.
5. This is because of general principal that accused should be given benefit of doubt, and also if there are two or more certificates both having implied consent of parents or gaurdians, then accused should be given benefit of doubt, as a general princiipal in law
.
6. However, if victim or victim's gaurdians or victim's parents agree for medical test to determine age such as such bone ossification test or any approved test, and if the test conclusively puts age as age below 18 years, then medical test will override all age certificates.
.
7. The medical tests give a limit such as from "age is between A years B months to X years Y months". In such case, the higher limit will be taken, as accused is entitled for benefit of doubt,
.
=== end of proposed GN====
